# Role: Code Reviewer

You are an expert code reviewer. Analyze code changes and provide actionable
feedback on quality, correctness, and maintainability.

Review checklist:
- Correctness: logic errors, off-by-one, null safety, edge cases
- Security: injection vulnerabilities, hardcoded secrets, unsafe operations
- Performance: unnecessary allocations, N+1 queries, missing indexes
- Style: naming conventions, code organization, DRY violations
- Error handling: unchecked exceptions, missing cleanup, resource leaks
- Tests: coverage gaps, flaky assertions, missing edge case tests
- API design: backwards compatibility, proper error codes, documentation

Use bash with `git diff`, `git log`, and `git show` to examine changes.
Use grep to check for patterns (e.g. find all callers of a changed method).
Read related test files to verify coverage.

Format your review as:
- **Critical** (must fix): bugs, security issues, data loss risks
- **Important** (should fix): performance, error handling, design issues
- **Minor** (nice to fix): style, naming, documentation
- **Positive**: good patterns, well-written code worth highlighting


## Context
Reviews code changes for bugs, style issues, security vulnerabilities, and improvements

---

Review the following set of fixes being made to address a VLM (SmolDocling) decode regression where the model outputs EOS on decode step 1 instead of generating text. The working commit was 11005b4ae6 (21 commits behind HEAD on branch ag_new_release_updates_2).

## Context
- SmolDocling VLM has NO external causal mask (causalMask=null, cachePosition=null)
- Uses standard KV concat mode in onnx_multi_head_attention op
- Uses `padKvToStaticSize` for static KV buffers from prefill
- The `onnx_multi_head_attention.cpp` changes between working and HEAD are the primary regression candidates

## Fixes Applied

### Fix 1: Restore AttentionWorkspace buffer in onnx_multi_head_attention.cpp
- **Problem**: HEAD switched from using `AttentionWorkspace::getInstance()->getBuffer()` + `nullify()` to writing FlashAttention output directly into a reshaped view of the output buffer
- **Risk**: If the output isn't C-contiguous (DSP plan-allocated), `reshape('c', ..., false)` creates a copy. The `directWrite` check tried to detect this but the double-reshape fallback path is fragile. More critically, the `nullify()` was removed — if FlashAttention doesn't write all positions (masked positions with attention bias), stale data leaks through
- **Fix**: Restored the workspace buffer pattern: `getBuffer()` → `nullify()` → FlashAttention writes into zeroed workspace → `reshape` + `assign()` back to output

### Fix 2: Restore syncToDevice() calls in KV concat path
- **Problem**: The working commit had `syncToDevice()` calls after all `assign()` operations on KV slices (kPastSlice, vPastSlice, kCurSlice, vCurSlice). These were removed in HEAD.
- **Investigation finding**: The `assign()` calls and FlashAttention both use the same CUDA stream (block.launchContext()->getCudaStream()), so stream ordering is fine. BUT syncToDevice() serves a different purpose: it flushes host-actual data to device. Permuted views from ext inputs (static KV buffers) may have host-actual data that `assign()` alone doesn't always transfer correctly.
- **Fix**: Added back `syncToDevice()` after each assign to KV slices in both the direct-write-to-present-output path and the fallback allocate-new-buffers path

### Fix 3: Remove validation-disabled-after-step-3 workaround in autoregressive_decode.cu
- **Problem**: All REQUIRE_TRUE validation checks (plan status, logits null, buffer closed, KV output indices, token ID range) were guarded by `if (step < 3)` — silently disabling runtime safety after 3 decode steps. This was a performance workaround.
- **Risk**: These are O(1) pointer/flag checks — negligible cost vs plan execution. Disabling them masks correctness bugs on steps 4+ and makes debugging impossible.
- **Fix**: Removed all `step < 3` guards — validation runs every step

### Fix 4: Add byte-capacity overrun guard for GDN/conv state feedback on CUDA
- **Problem**: The CPU path has `srcDb->getLenInBytes() > dstDb->getLenInBytes()` guard before copy. The CUDA path uses raw `cudaMemcpyAsync` with only `lengthOf()` and `dataType()` checks — missing the byte-capacity check that prevents overwriting past the destination DataBuffer.
- **Fix**: Added the same `getLenInBytes()` guard that CPU has before `cudaMemcpyAsync` for both GDN state and conv state feedback loops

### Fix 5: Removed dead code (qContig/kContig/vContig unused variables)

## Questions for Review

1. Is restoring AttentionWorkspace the right fix? Could the direct-write approach work correctly with additional guards (e.g., explicit contiguity check + nullify before FlashAttention)?
2. Are the syncToDevice() calls truly needed given that all ops use the same stream? Could there be a case where assign() on a non-contiguous view with host-actual source data fails to properly transfer?
3. Is there any performance concern with running REQUIRE_TRUE on every decode step (250+ tokens)?
4. Are there other changes between 11005b4ae6 and HEAD that could cause the regression that we haven't addressed?
5. The GenerationPipeline.java diff shows a `logitsName` default change from "logits" to "lm_logits" — could this affect SmolDocling?

Please assess the overall fix strategy and highlight any gaps or risks.

    21	//
    22	// ONNX MultiHeadAttention op - takes pre-projected Q, K, V
    23	// Compatible with Microsoft's ONNX MultiHeadAttention operator

    25	
    26	#include <system/op_boilerplate.h>

    34	
    35	namespace sd {
    36	namespace ops {
    37	
    38	/**
    39	 * ONNX MultiHeadAttention - for pre-projected queries, keys, values
    40	 *
    41	 * Inputs:
    42	 *   0: query          [batch, seqQ, hidden] - already projected
    43	 *   1: key            [batch, seqKV, hidden] - already projected
    44	 *   2: value          [batch, seqKV, hidden] - already projected
    45	 *   3: attn_bias      [batch, numHeads, seqQ, seqKV] or broadcastable (optional, can be empty)
    46	 *   4: past_key       [batch, numHeads, pastSeq, headDim] (optional, can be empty)
    47	 *   5: past_value     [batch, numHeads, pastSeq, headDim] (optional, can be empty)
    48	 *   6: cache_position [1] INT64 scalar (optional) - when present, enables in-place KV write mode:
    49	 *        writes current K/V at this position in past_key/past_value (in-place),
    50	 *        uses past_key/past_value as the full KV sequence (no concatenation).
    51	 *        This fixes causal mask alignment: the model's causal mask uses position_ids
    52	 *        to determine visibility, so current K must be at the same position as position_ids.
    53	 *        Without this, concat places current K at pastSeq which is > position_ids, causing
    54	 *        the causal mask to mask out the current token's own key.
    55	 *
    56	 * Int args:
    57	 *   0: numHeads
    58	 *   1: useCausalMask (0 or 1)
    59	 *
    60	 * Float args:
    61	 *   0: scale (0 = auto compute 1/sqrt(headDim))
    62	 *
    63	 * Outputs:
    64	 *   0: output         [batch, seqQ, hidden]
    65	 *   1: present_key    [batch, numHeads, totalSeq, headDim] (optional)
    66	 *   2: present_value  [batch, numHeads, totalSeq, headDim] (optional)
    67	 */
    68	CUSTOM_OP_IMPL(onnx_multi_head_attention, 3, -1, false, -2, 2) {
    69	  auto query = INPUT_VARIABLE(0);   // [batch, seqQ, hidden]
    70	  auto key = INPUT_VARIABLE(1);     // [batch, seqKV, hidden]
    71	  auto value = INPUT_VARIABLE(2);   // [batch, seqKV, hidden]
    72	  
    73	  NDArray* attnBias = block.width() > 3 ? INPUT_VARIABLE(3) : nullptr;
    74	  NDArray* pastKey = block.width() > 4 ? INPUT_VARIABLE(4) : nullptr;
    75	  NDArray* pastValue = block.width() > 5 ? INPUT_VARIABLE(5) : nullptr;

    80	  if (attnBias != nullptr && (attnBias->isEmpty() || attnBias->rankOf() == 0 || attnBias->lengthOf() <= 1)) attnBias = nullptr;

    82	  if (pastValue != nullptr && (pastValue->isEmpty() || pastValue->rankOf() == 0 || pastValue->lengthOf() <= 1)) pastValue = nullptr;
    83	  // cache_position must be a non-empty scalar or 1-element tensor with value >= 0
    84	  if (cachePosInput != nullptr && (cachePosInput->isEmpty() || cachePosInput->lengthOf() <= 0)) cachePosInput = nullptr;
    85	  bool useInPlaceKv = (cachePosInput != nullptr && pastKey != nullptr && pastValue != nullptr);
    86	  
    87	  auto output = OUTPUT_VARIABLE(0);
    88	
    89	  LongType numHeads = INT_ARG(0);

    91	
    92	  double scale = block.numT() > 0 ? T_ARG(0) : 0.0;
    93	
    94	  // Handle empty K/V inputs — no key-value pairs to attend to.

    97	  if (key->isEmpty() || key->lengthOf() == 0 || value->isEmpty() || value->lengthOf() == 0) {
    98	    output->nullify();
    99	    if (block.outputWidth() > 1) OUTPUT_VARIABLE(1)->nullify();
   100	    if (block.outputWidth() > 2) OUTPUT_VARIABLE(2)->nullify();
   101	    return sd::Status::OK;
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  // Mixed-type auto-cast: cast all inputs to query's dtype.

   106	  // IMPORTANT: save original pastKey/pastValue pointers BEFORE casting.
   107	  // kvInPlaceWrite must target the real persistent KV cache buffers,
   108	  // not cast temporaries that get deleted at end of op.
   109	  DataType targetType = query->dataType();

   111	  NDArray* valueCast = nullptr;
   112	  NDArray* pastKeyCast = nullptr;
   113	  NDArray* pastValueCast = nullptr;

   115	  NDArray* origPastValue = pastValue;

   119	  }

   121	    valueCast = value->cast(targetType);
   122	    value = valueCast;
   123	  }
   124	  if (pastKey && pastKey->dataType() != targetType) {

   126	    pastKey = pastKeyCast;
   127	  }
   128	  if (pastValue && pastValue->dataType() != targetType) {

   130	    pastValue = pastValueCast;

   133	  auto batch = query->sizeAt(0);
   134	  auto seqQ = query->sizeAt(1);

   136	  auto seqKV = key->sizeAt(1);
   137	  auto kvHidden = key->sizeAt(2);

   140	  REQUIRE_TRUE(query->rankOf() == 3, 0,

   145	               ShapeUtils::shapeAsString(key).c_str());

   147	               "onnx_multi_head_attention: value must be rank 3, got %s",
   148	               ShapeUtils::shapeAsString(value).c_str());

   167	  }

   169	  // Reshape [batch, seq, hidden] -> [batch, seq, heads, headDim] (BSHD format for FlashAttentionHelper)

   171	  // Force contiguous copy before reshape: DSP pre-allocated arrays may have non-standard
   172	  // strides from view ops or max-allocation. reshape('c', ..., false) creates views that
   173	  // map coordinates using the source's strides — if strides differ between DSP and standard

   178	
   179	  NDArray* qReshaped = query->reshape('c', qShape4d, false);
   180	  NDArray* kReshaped = key->reshape('c', kvShape4d, false);
   181	  NDArray* vReshaped = value->reshape('c', kvShape4d, false);
   182	  
   183	  // Handle past key/value concatenation
   184	  // Use pointers and track ownership
   185	  NDArray* kFinal = nullptr;
   186	  NDArray* vFinal = nullptr;

   188	  bool skipPresentOutput = false;  // True when present output was written in-place
   189	  LongType totalSeqKV = seqKV;

   191	  // Skip past_key concat when the upstream graph already handled it.
   192	  // Detection: if past_key's head count (axis 1) doesn't match numKvHeads derived from

   202	    //
   203	    // CUDA graph compatible: kvInPlaceWrite reads cache_position from a device-side
   204	    // pointer. The pointer ADDRESS is baked into the graph at capture time; only
   205	    // the VALUE changes between replays (updated via cudaMemcpyAsync before replay).
   206	    // The old code used cachePosInput->e<LongType>(0) which bakes the HOST VALUE
   207	    // into the graph — broken on replay.
   208	    //
   209	    // We write to pastKey/pastValue (ext inputs = persistent static KV buffers)
   210	    // rather than presentKey/presentValue (plan outputs) because:
   211	    // (a) past buffers have stable addresses across CUDA graph replays
   212	    // (b) the next step reads from past, not present
   213	    // (c) present outputs may be prezeroed on some plan paths
   214	
   215	    // pastKey is BHSD [batch, numKvHeads, maxSeqLen, headDim]
   216	    // kReshaped is BSHD [batch, seqKV, numKvHeads, headDim]

   218	
   219	    // Use specialBuffer on CUDA (device pointer), buffer on CPU (host pointer)
   220	#if defined(SD_CUDA)
   221	    const void* cachePosPtr = cachePosInput->specialBuffer();
   222	#else
   223	    const void* cachePosPtr = cachePosInput->buffer();
   224	#endif

   226	    // Write into the ORIGINAL persistent KV cache buffers, not cast temporaries.
   227	    // If pastKey was cast to a different dtype, origPastKey points to the real cache.
   228	    helpers::kvInPlaceWrite(origPastKey, kReshaped, cachePosPtr, block.launchContext());
   229	    helpers::kvInPlaceWrite(origPastValue, vReshaped, cachePosPtr, block.launchContext());
   230	
   231	    // Use the ORIGINAL pastKey/pastValue for attention (permute BHSD→BSHD).
   232	    // totalSeqKV = maxSeqLen: the causal mask handles token visibility,
   233	    // so we pass the full buffer. Positions beyond cachePos+seqKV contain
   234	    // zeros or stale data but are masked out by the attention mask.
   235	    totalSeqKV = origPastKey->sizeAt(2);  // maxSeqLen
   236	
   237	    std::vector<LongType> permBHSDtoBSHD = {0, 2, 1, 3};
   238	    kFinal = origPastKey->permute(permBHSDtoBSHD, false, false);
   239	    vFinal = origPastValue->permute(permBHSDtoBSHD, false, false);

   301	      vFinal = new NDArray('c', finalShape, value->dataType(), block.launchContext());

   303	
   304	      std::vector<LongType> pastSliceIdx = {0, batch, 0, pastSeq, 0, numKvHeads, 0, headDim};

   314	      delete vPastSlice;

   316	      NDArray* kCurSlice = (*kFinal)(curSliceIdx);

   319	      vCurSlice->assign(vReshaped);

   326	      delete pastValueBSHD;

   366	  // Output in BSHD format [batch, seqQ, numHeads, headDim]

   405	  // Clean up
   406	  if (ownKVFinal) {

   408	    delete vFinal;
   409	  }
   410	  delete qReshaped;
   411	  delete kReshaped;
   412	  delete vReshaped;
   413	
   414	  delete keyCast;
   415	  delete valueCast;
   416	  delete pastKeyCast;
   417	  delete pastValueCast;
   418	
   419	  return sd::Status::OK;
   420	}

   422	DECLARE_TYPES(onnx_multi_head_attention) {
   423	  getOpDescriptor()
   424	      ->setAllowedInputTypes(0, {ALL_FLOATS})   // query
   425	      ->setAllowedInputTypes(1, {ALL_FLOATS})   // key
   426	      ->setAllowedInputTypes(2, {ALL_FLOATS})   // value

   429	      ->setAllowedInputTypes(5, {ALL_FLOATS})   // past_value (optional)
   430	      ->setAllowedInputTypes(6, {ALL_INTS})     // cache_position (optional, INT64 scalar)
   431	      ->setAllowedOutputTypes({ALL_FLOATS})
   432	      ->addTraits(OP_TRAIT_ATTENTION | OP_TRAIT_FULLY_WRITING);

   480	
   481	        // ── Step 1: Update plan external inputs for this decode step ──
   482	        // decodeEmbedding IS prefillEmbeddings (same NDArray, same device address).
   483	        // The embed lookup kernel writes into it in-place each step, keeping the
   484	        // device address stable for CUDA graph replay (externalAddrsMatch).
   485	        if (config->embeddingsExtIdx >= 0 && config->embeddingsExtIdx < numExtInputs) {
   486	            extInputs[config->embeddingsExtIdx] = decodeEmbedding;
   487	        }
   488	
   489	        // Attention mask
   490	        if (config->maskExtIdx >= 0 && config->maskExtIdx < numExtInputs) {
   491	            extInputs[config->maskExtIdx] = attentionMask;
   492	        }
   493	
   494	        // Position IDs
   495	        if (config->posIdsExtIdx >= 0 && config->posIdsExtIdx < numExtInputs) {
   496	            extInputs[config->posIdsExtIdx] = positionIds;
   497	        }
   498	
   499	        // Input IDs
   500	        if (config->inputIdsExtIdx >= 0 && config->inputIdsExtIdx < numExtInputs) {
   501	            extInputs[config->inputIdsExtIdx] = inputIds;
   502	        }
   503	
   504	        // Causal mask: wire into ext inputs (same pointer every step, updated in-place)
   505	        if (causalMask != nullptr && config->causalMaskExtIdx >= 0 && config->causalMaskExtIdx < numExtInputs) {
   506	            extInputs[config->causalMaskExtIdx] = causalMask;
   507	        }
   508	
   509	        // KV cache inputs: point to static buffers
   510	        if (config->kvInputExtIndices != nullptr && staticKvBuffers != nullptr) {
   511	            for (int kv = 0; kv < 2 * numKvPairs; kv++) {
   512	                int kvIdx = config->kvInputExtIndices[kv];
   513	                if (kvIdx >= 0 && kvIdx < numExtInputs) {
   514	                    extInputs[kvIdx] = staticKvBuffers[kv];
   515	                }
   516	            }
   517	        }
   518	
   519	
   520	        // ── Step 1b: Pre-unmask the CURRENT position in causal mask ──
   521	        // The attention op writes KV at cache_position = currentPosition, then attends
   522	        // to the full buffer including that position. If the causal mask masks the current
   523	        // position, the token can't attend to its own KV entry → wrong attention output.
   524	        // The post-execution mask update (below) only unmasks for the NEXT step.
   525	        if (causalMask != nullptr && currentPosition >= 0 && currentPosition < causalMaskLen) {
   526	            NDArray::prepareSpecialUse({causalMask}, {});
   527	            BUILD_SINGLE_SELECTOR(causalMask->dataType(), updateCausalMaskLauncher,
   528	                                  (stream, causalMask->specialBuffer(), currentPosition, causalMaskLen),
   529	                                  SD_FLOAT_TYPES);
   530	            NDArray::registerSpecialUse({causalMask}, {});
   531	        }
   532	        if (attnMaskReformat != nullptr && currentPosition >= 0 && currentPosition < attnMaskReformatLen) {
   533	            NDArray::prepareSpecialUse({attnMaskReformat}, {});
   534	            BUILD_SINGLE_SELECTOR(attnMaskReformat->dataType(), updateCausalMaskLauncher,
   535	                                  (stream, attnMaskReformat->specialBuffer(), currentPosition, attnMaskReformatLen),
   536	                                  SD_FLOAT_TYPES);
   537	            NDArray::registerSpecialUse({attnMaskReformat}, {});
   538	        }
   539	        // Also unmask the attention mask (0/1 mask)
   540	        if (currentPosition >= 0 && currentPosition < maxKvLen) {
   541	            NDArray::prepareSpecialUse({attentionMask}, {});
   542	            BUILD_SINGLE_SELECTOR(attentionMask->dataType(), updateAttentionMaskLauncher,
   543	                                  (stream, attentionMask->specialBuffer(), currentPosition, maxKvLen),
   544	                                  SD_COMMON_TYPES);
   545	            NDArray::registerSpecialUse({attentionMask}, {});
   546	        }
   547	
   548	        auto tWireEnd = stepTimingEnabled ? std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now() : stepStart;
   549	
   550	        // ── Step 2: Execute plan ──
   551	        if (step < 5 && env_isVerbose()) {
   552	            // Trace ext input states before plan execution
   553	            auto* embDb = decodeEmbedding->dataBuffer();
   554	            auto* maskDb = attentionMask->dataBuffer();
   555	            auto* posDb = positionIds->dataBuffer();
   556	            sd_printf("DECODE_STEP[%d]: PRE_EXEC embPrimAct=%d embSpecAct=%d "
   557	                      "maskPrimAct=%d maskSpecAct=%d posPrimAct=%d posSpecAct=%d "
   558	                      "embSpecBuf=%p maskSpecBuf=%p posSpecBuf=%p\n",
   559	                      step,
   560	                      embDb ? embDb->isPrimaryActual() : -1,
   561	                      embDb ? embDb->isSpecialActual() : -1,
   562	                      maskDb ? maskDb->isPrimaryActual() : -1,
   563	                      maskDb ? maskDb->isSpecialActual() : -1,
   564	                      posDb ? posDb->isPrimaryActual() : -1,
   565	                      posDb ? posDb->isSpecialActual() : -1,
   566	                      decodeEmbedding->specialBuffer(),
   567	                      attentionMask->specialBuffer(),
   568	                      positionIds->specialBuffer());
   569	        }
   570	        // Use executeSteadyState() for the hot path — it skips ~200ms of
   571	        // per-step CPU overhead (lifecycle validation, buffer scanning,
   572	        // fingerprinting, diagnostics). Falls back to full execute()
   573	        // automatically if the plan hasn't reached steady state yet.
   574	        Status planStatus = plan->executeSteadyState(
   575	            extInputs, numExtInputs,
   576	            planOutputs, numPlanOutputs,
   577	            reinterpret_cast<void*>(const_cast<cudaStream_t*>(stream)));
   578	
   579	        // Validate plan output every step — these are O(1) pointer/flag checks,
   580	        // negligible cost compared to the plan execution itself.
   581	        REQUIRE_TRUE(planStatus == Status::OK, 0,
   582	                     "autoregressive_decode: plan execution FAILED at step %d with status %d. "
   583	                     "Plan state: frozen=%d numExt=%d numOutputs=%d. "
   584	                     "This is NOT recoverable — fix the plan execution failure.",
   585	                     step, static_cast<int>(planStatus),
   586	                     plan->isShapesFrozen() ? 1 : 0,
   587	                     numExtInputs, numPlanOutputs);
   588	
   589	        REQUIRE_TRUE(config->logitsOutputIdx >= 0 && config->logitsOutputIdx < numPlanOutputs, 0,
   590	                     "autoregressive_decode: logitsOutputIdx=%d out of range [0,%d) at step %d. "
   591	                     "The plan has fewer outputs than expected or logitsOutputIdx was not set.",
   592	                     config->logitsOutputIdx, numPlanOutputs, step);
   593	        REQUIRE_TRUE(planOutputs[config->logitsOutputIdx] != nullptr, 0,
   594	                     "autoregressive_decode: logits output NDArray* is null at step %d (idx=%d). "
   595	                     "Plan returned OK but did not populate the logits output slot.",
   596	                     step, config->logitsOutputIdx);
   597	
   598	        {
   599	            NDArray* logitsArr = planOutputs[config->logitsOutputIdx];
   600	            auto* logitsDb = logitsArr->dataBuffer();
   601	            REQUIRE_TRUE(logitsDb != nullptr, 0,
   602	                         "autoregressive_decode: logits DataBuffer is null at step %d. "
   603	                         "Output array exists but has no backing buffer — likely a stale slot.",
   604	                         step);
   605	            REQUIRE_TRUE(!logitsDb->isClosed(), 0,
   606	                         "autoregressive_decode: logits DataBuffer is CLOSED at step %d. "
   607	                         "The plan reused a freed buffer — stale slot reuse bug.",
   608	                         step);
   609	            REQUIRE_TRUE(logitsArr->specialBuffer() != nullptr, 0,
   610	                         "autoregressive_decode: logits specialBuffer (device ptr) is null at step %d. "
   611	                         "Buffer exists but has no device allocation — missing syncToDevice or stale buffer.",
   612	                         step);
   613	        }
   614	
   615	        // NOTE: Do NOT call plan->setShapesFrozen(true) here.
   616	        // The plan auto-seals during its first execute() call, which sets
   617	        // shapesFrozen=true and triggers Triton compilation. Calling
   618	        // setShapesFrozen after execute violates the plan lifecycle
   619	        // (executeCount > 0) and would skip the warmup/capture phase.
   620	        // Auto-seal handles the transition correctly.
   621	
   622	        auto tPlanEnd = stepTimingEnabled ? std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now() : stepStart;
   623	
   624	        // ── Step 2b: GDN/conv recurrent state feedback ──
   625	        // Copy state outputs back to ext inputs for the next decode step.
   626	        // Critical for hybrid architectures (e.g. Qwen with GDN layers).
   627	        // Without this, GDN layers see frozen state from warmup and degenerate.
   628	        if (config->numGdnStatePairs > 0 && config->gdnStateExtIndices != nullptr
   629	            && config->gdnStateOutputIndices != nullptr) {
   630	            for (int s = 0; s < config->numGdnStatePairs; s++) {
   631	                int outIdx = config->gdnStateOutputIndices[s];
   632	                int extIdx = config->gdnStateExtIndices[s];
   633	                if (outIdx >= 0 && outIdx < numPlanOutputs && planOutputs[outIdx] != nullptr
   634	                    && extIdx >= 0 && extIdx < numExtInputs && extInputs[extIdx] != nullptr) {
   635	                    NDArray* src = planOutputs[outIdx];
   636	                    NDArray* dst = extInputs[extIdx];
   637	                    if (src->lengthOf() == dst->lengthOf() && src->dataType() == dst->dataType()) {
   638	                        size_t bytes = src->lengthOf() * src->sizeOfT();
   639	                        auto* srcDb = src->dataBuffer();
   640	                        auto* dstDb = dst->dataBuffer();
   641	                        if (srcDb != nullptr && dstDb != nullptr &&
   642	                            srcDb->getLenInBytes() > dstDb->getLenInBytes()) {
   643	                            continue;  // Prevent buffer overrun
   644	                        }
   645	                        cudaMemcpyAsync(dst->specialBuffer(), src->specialBuffer(),
   646	                                        bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, *stream);
   647	                        dst->tickWriteDevice();
   648	                    }
   649	                }
   650	            }
   651	        }
   652	        if (config->numConvStatePairs > 0 && config->convStateExtIndices != nullptr
   653	            && config->convStateOutputIndices != nullptr) {
   654	            for (int s = 0; s < config->numConvStatePairs; s++) {
   655	                int outIdx = config->convStateOutputIndices[s];
   656	                int extIdx = config->convStateExtIndices[s];
   657	                if (outIdx >= 0 && outIdx < numPlanOutputs && planOutputs[outIdx] != nullptr
   658	                    && extIdx >= 0 && extIdx < numExtInputs && extInputs[extIdx] != nullptr) {
   659	                    NDArray* src = planOutputs[outIdx];
   660	                    NDArray* dst = extInputs[extIdx];
   661	                    if (src->lengthOf() == dst->lengthOf() && src->dataType() == dst->dataType()) {
   662	                        size_t bytes = src->lengthOf() * src->sizeOfT();
   663	                        auto* srcDb = src->dataBuffer();
   664	                        auto* dstDb = dst->dataBuffer();
   665	                        if (srcDb != nullptr && dstDb != nullptr &&
   666	                            srcDb->getLenInBytes() > dstDb->getLenInBytes()) {
   667	                            continue;  // Prevent buffer overrun
   668	                        }
   669	                        cudaMemcpyAsync(dst->specialBuffer(), src->specialBuffer(),
   670	                                        bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, *stream);
   671	                        dst->tickWriteDevice();
   672	                    }
   673	                }
   674	            }
   675	        }
   676	
   677	        // ── Step 3: Token sampling ──
   678	        // Get logits from plan output at config->logitsOutputIdx
   679	        NDArray* logitsOutput = planOutputs[config->logitsOutputIdx];
   680	
   681	        // logitsOutput shape: [1, seqLen, vocabSize]
   682	        // For decode steps (seqLen=1), it's [1, 1, vocabSize] — take [0, 0, :]
   683	        // For prefill (seqLen=N), take [0, N-1, :] (last position)
   684	        LongType logitsSeqLen = logitsOutput->sizeAt(1);
   685	        LongType logitsVocab = logitsOutput->sizeAt(2);
   686	
   687	        // Get pointer to last-position logits (already on device)
   688	        NDArray::prepareSpecialUse({sampledToken}, {logitsOutput});
   689	
   690	        // ── Trace: dump logits fingerprint for debugging ──
   691	        if (step < 10 && env_isVerbose()) {
   692	            cudaStreamSynchronize(*stream);
   693	            float logitsFingerprint[4] = {0};
   694	            LongType lastPosOff = (logitsSeqLen - 1) * logitsVocab;
   695	            const void* logitsDbg = static_cast<const char*>(logitsOutput->specialBuffer())
   696	                                    + lastPosOff * logitsOutput->sizeOfT();
   697	            if (logitsOutput->dataType() == DataType::FLOAT32) {
   698	                cudaMemcpy(logitsFingerprint, logitsDbg, sizeof(float) * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost);
   699	            }
   700	            sd_printf("DECODE_STEP[%d]: logitsShape=[%lld,%lld,%lld] vocab=%lld "
   701	                      "logits[0..3]=[%.4f,%.4f,%.4f,%.4f] specialBuf=%p\n",
   702	                      step, logitsOutput->sizeAt(0), logitsOutput->sizeAt(1), logitsOutput->sizeAt(2),
   703	                      logitsVocab, logitsFingerprint[0], logitsFingerprint[1],
   704	                      logitsFingerprint[2], logitsFingerprint[3],
   705	                      logitsOutput->specialBuffer());
   706	        }
   707	
   708	        if (temperature <= 0.0 || (topK <= 1 && topP <= 0.0)) {
   709	            // Greedy: argmax over last-position logits
   710	            REQUIRE_TRUE(logitsVocab > 0, 0,
   711	                         "autoregressive_decode: logits vocab dimension is 0 at step %d. "
   712	                         "Cannot perform argmax on empty vocabulary.",
   713	                         step);
   714	            // Compute offset to last position: (logitsSeqLen-1) * vocabSize
   715	            LongType lastPosOffset = (logitsSeqLen - 1) * logitsVocab;
   716	            const void* logitsPtr = static_cast<const char*>(logitsOutput->specialBuffer())
   717	                                    + lastPosOffset * logitsOutput->sizeOfT();
   718	
   719	            BUILD_SINGLE_SELECTOR(logitsOutput->dataType(), argmaxLauncher,
   720	                                  (stream, logitsPtr, sampledToken->specialBuffer(), logitsVocab),
   721	                                  SD_COMMON_TYPES);
   722	        } else {
   723	            // Sampling: use tokenSampleCuda with greedy fallback on logits pointer
   724	            // For sampling, we pass the full logits output (tokenSampleCuda handles last-pos extraction)
   725	            tokenSampleCuda(logitsOutput, sampledToken, temperature, topK, topP,
   726	                            static_cast<LongType>(step), context);
   727	        }
   728	
   729	        NDArray::registerSpecialUse({sampledToken}, {logitsOutput});
   730	
   731	        // ── Tier 1a: Store token via D2D copy (avoids p() hidden H2D + stream 0 sync) ──
   732	        // generatedTokenIds->p() does host write → syncToDevice() → cudaMemcpyAsync
   733	        // on stream 0 + cudaStreamSynchronize(stream_0) — a hidden pipeline drain.
   734	        // Direct D2D from sampledToken to generatedTokenIds stays on the decode stream.
   735	        {
   736	            void* dstPtr = static_cast<char*>(generatedTokenIds->specialBuffer())
   737	                           + tokensGenerated * sizeof(LongType);
   738	            cudaMemcpyAsync(dstPtr, sampledToken->specialBuffer(),
   739	                            sizeof(LongType), cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, *stream);
   740	            generatedTokenIds->tickWriteDevice();
   741	        }
   742	        tokensGenerated++;
   743	
   744	        // ── Tier 1b: Launch mask/position updates BEFORE the sync ──
   745	        // These kernels only depend on currentPosition (CPU counter), NOT nextTokenId.
   746	        // Launching them here overlaps their GPU execution with the graph replay that's
   747	        // still in flight on the stream, and hides their latency behind the sync wait.
   748	        //
   749	        // Advance position BEFORE updating mask/posIds for the next step.
   750	        // KV scatter (step 5, after sync) used currentPosition as the cache write position.
   751	        // Now increment so the mask/posIds updates prepare the NEXT decode step's inputs.
   752	        currentPosition++;
   753	
   754	        // Update attention mask: unmask the KV position that was JUST written.
   755	        LongType kvJustWritten = currentPosition - 1;
   756	        if (kvJustWritten >= 0 && kvJustWritten < maxKvLen) {
   757	            NDArray::prepareSpecialUse({attentionMask}, {});
   758	            BUILD_SINGLE_SELECTOR(attentionMask->dataType(), updateAttentionMaskLauncher,
   759	                                  (stream, attentionMask->specialBuffer(), kvJustWritten, maxKvLen),
   760	                                  SD_COMMON_TYPES);
   761	            NDArray::registerSpecialUse({attentionMask}, {});
   762	        }
   763	
   764	        // Update causal mask: unmask the KV position that was JUST written (set to 0.0f)
   765	        if (causalMask != nullptr && kvJustWritten >= 0 && kvJustWritten < causalMaskLen) {
   766	            NDArray::prepareSpecialUse({causalMask}, {});
   767	            BUILD_SINGLE_SELECTOR(causalMask->dataType(), updateCausalMaskLauncher,
   768	                                  (stream, causalMask->specialBuffer(), kvJustWritten, causalMaskLen),
   769	                                  SD_FLOAT_TYPES);
   770	            NDArray::registerSpecialUse({causalMask}, {});
   771	        }
   772	
   773	        // Update attn_mask_reformat: unmask the just-written KV position (set to 0.0f)
   774	        if (attnMaskReformat != nullptr && kvJustWritten >= 0 && kvJustWritten < attnMaskReformatLen) {
   775	            NDArray::prepareSpecialUse({attnMaskReformat}, {});
   776	            BUILD_SINGLE_SELECTOR(attnMaskReformat->dataType(), updateCausalMaskLauncher,
   777	                                  (stream, attnMaskReformat->specialBuffer(), kvJustWritten, attnMaskReformatLen),
   778	                                  SD_FLOAT_TYPES);
   779	            NDArray::registerSpecialUse({attnMaskReformat}, {});
   780	        }
   781	
   782	        // Update position_ids: set to next step's position
   783	        NDArray::prepareSpecialUse({positionIds}, {});
   784	        updatePositionIdsKernel<<<1, 1, 0, *stream>>>(
   785	            positionIds->specialBuffer(),
   786	            currentPosition);
   787	        NDArray::registerSpecialUse({positionIds}, {});
   788	
   789	        // ── Tier 1c: D2H token readback via pinned memory ──
   790	        // Read sampled token ID back to host (single int64).
   791	        // Issue D2H copy on the SAME stream as the argmax kernel — FIFO ordering
   792	        // guarantees the copy starts after argmax completes.
   793	        // Using pinned memory enables true async DMA (no driver bounce buffer).
   794	        LongType* tokenDst = pinnedTokenId ? pinnedTokenId : &stackTokenId;
   795	        *tokenDst = 0;
   796	        auto tSyncStart = stepTimingEnabled ? std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now() : stepStart;
   797	        cudaMemcpyAsync(tokenDst, sampledToken->specialBuffer(),
   798	                        sizeof(LongType), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, *stream);
   799	        cudaStreamSynchronize(*stream);
   800	        auto tSyncEnd = stepTimingEnabled ? std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now() : stepStart;
   801	        LongType nextTokenId = *tokenDst;
   802	
   803	        if (step < 5 && env_isVerbose()) {
   804	            sd_printf("DECODE_STEP[%d]: nextTokenId=%lld currentPosition=%lld\n",
   805	                      step, (long long)nextTokenId, (long long)currentPosition);
   806	        }
   807	
   808	        // ── Step 4: Check stop condition ──
   809	        bool shouldStop = false;
   810	        for (int s : stopTokenIds) {
   811	            if (nextTokenId == static_cast<LongType>(s)) {
   812	                shouldStop = true;
   813	                break;
   814	            }
   815	        }
   816	
   817	        auto tStopCheck = stepTimingEnabled ? std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now() : stepStart;
   818	
   819	        // Compute step time using the stop check timestamp (before KV scatter/embed updates)
   820	        double stepMs = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(tStopCheck - stepStart).count();
   821	        stepTimesMs.push_back(stepMs);
   822	
   823	        if (shouldStop) break;
   824	
   825	        // ── Step 5: KV scatter — copy present KV into static buffers ──
   826	        // Skip manual scatter when the plan's native KV scatter is active
   827	        // (planOwnsKvScatter) — executeKvScatterPostExec handles it with its
   828	        // own device-side position counter via executeSteadyState.
   829	        if (!config->planOwnsKvScatter &&
   830	            config->kvOutputIndices != nullptr && staticKvBuffers != nullptr && numKvPairs > 0) {
   831	            // Build batched KV scatter entries.
   832	            std::vector<KvScatterEntry> entries(2 * numKvPairs);
   833	            for (int kv = 0; kv < 2 * numKvPairs; kv++) {
   834	                int kvOutIdx = config->kvOutputIndices[kv];
   835	                NDArray* presentKv = planOutputs[kvOutIdx];
   836	                NDArray* staticBuf = staticKvBuffers[kv];
   837	
   838	                REQUIRE_TRUE(kvOutIdx >= 0 && kvOutIdx < numPlanOutputs, 0,
   839	                             "autoregressive_decode: KV output index %d out of range [0,%d) "
   840	                             "at step %d kv=%d",
   841	                             kvOutIdx, numPlanOutputs, step, kv);
   842	                REQUIRE_TRUE(presentKv != nullptr, 0,
   843	                             "autoregressive_decode: KV output[%d] (planOutput[%d]) is null "
   844	                             "at step %d — plan did not produce this output.",
   845	                             kv, kvOutIdx, step);
   846	                REQUIRE_TRUE(staticBuf != nullptr, 0,
   847	                             "autoregressive_decode: static KV buffer[%d] is null at step %d.",
   848	                             kv, step);
   849	                REQUIRE_TRUE(presentKv->specialBuffer() != nullptr, 0,
   850	                             "autoregressive_decode: KV output[%d] has null device buffer "
   851	                             "at step %d — stale or uninitialized output.",
   852	                             kv, step);
   853	                REQUIRE_TRUE(staticBuf->specialBuffer() != nullptr, 0,
   854	                             "autoregressive_decode: static KV[%d] has null device buffer "
   855	                             "at step %d — buffer was freed or never allocated.",
   856	                             kv, step);
   857	
   858	                entries[kv].srcPtr = presentKv->specialBuffer();
   859	                entries[kv].dstPtr = staticBuf->specialBuffer();
   860	                entries[kv].heads = presentKv->sizeAt(1);
   861	                entries[kv].srcSeqLen = presentKv->sizeAt(2);
   862	                entries[kv].dstSeqLen = staticBuf->sizeAt(2);
   863	                entries[kv].dim = presentKv->sizeAt(3);
   864	                entries[kv].lastPos = presentKv->sizeAt(2) - 1;
   865	                entries[kv].cachePos = currentPosition - 1;  // pre-increment: use previous position
   866	            }
   867	
   868	            REQUIRE_TRUE(staticKvBuffers[0] != nullptr, 0,
   869	                         "autoregressive_decode: staticKvBuffers[0] is null at step %d — "
   870	                         "cannot determine KV data type for scatter.",
   871	                         step);
   872	            kvScatterBatched(entries.data(), 2 * numKvPairs,
   873	                             staticKvBuffers[0]->dataType(), context);
   874	
   875	            // Mark static KV buffers as device-authoritative after scatter.
   876	            for (int kv = 0; kv < 2 * numKvPairs; kv++) {
   877	                staticKvBuffers[kv]->tickWriteDevice();
   878	            }
   879	        }
   880	
   881	        // ── Step 6: Embedding lookup for next token ──
   882	        // Only perform embedding lookup if we have an embeddings ext input to update.
   883	        // In single-model mode (embeddingsExtIdx == -1), the model handles its own
   884	        // embedding lookup internally, so we skip this step.
   885	        if (config->embeddingsExtIdx >= 0) {
   886	            REQUIRE_TRUE(nextTokenId >= 0 && nextTokenId < vocabSize, 0,
   887	                         "autoregressive_decode: nextTokenId=%lld out of range [0,%lld) at step %d. "
   888	                         "Argmax/sampling returned an invalid token ID.",
   889	                         (long long)nextTokenId, (long long)vocabSize, step);
   890	            NDArray::prepareSpecialUse({decodeEmbedding}, {embeddingTable});
   891	            BUILD_SINGLE_SELECTOR(embeddingTable->dataType(), embedLookupLauncher,
   892	                                  (stream, embeddingTable->specialBuffer(),
   893	                                   decodeEmbedding->specialBuffer(),
   894	                                   nextTokenId, hidden, embTableRowStride),
   895	                                  SD_COMMON_TYPES);
   896	            NDArray::registerSpecialUse({decodeEmbedding}, {embeddingTable});
   897	        }
   898	
   899	        // Update input_ids: set to next token (needs nextTokenId from D2H)
   900	        NDArray::prepareSpecialUse({inputIds}, {});
   901	        updateInputIdsKernel<<<1, 1, 0, *stream>>>(
   902	            inputIds->specialBuffer(),
   903	            nextTokenId);
   904	        NDArray::registerSpecialUse({inputIds}, {});
   905	
   906	        // ── Update in-graph KV cache scalars (GGUF pattern) ──
   907	        // position_offset and cache_position are scalar ext inputs that the
   908	        // attention op reads for RoPE position and KV write position.
   909	        if (config->positionOffsetExtIdx >= 0 && config->positionOffsetExtIdx < numExtInputs) {
   910	            NDArray* posOffset = extInputs[config->positionOffsetExtIdx];

  1272	// (X * Y) = Z[0]
  1273	NDArray* MmulHelper::dot(NDArray* X, NDArray* Y, NDArray* Z, const double alpha, const double beta) {
  1274	 LongType xLenDim(0), yLenDim(0);
  1275	
  1276	 if (!shape::isCommonVector(X->shapeInfo(), xLenDim))
  1277	   THROW_EXCEPTION("MmulHelper::dot cuda: X array must be vector !");
  1278	 if (!shape::isCommonVector(Y->shapeInfo(), yLenDim))
  1279	   THROW_EXCEPTION("MmulHelper::dot cuda: Y array must be vector !");

  1281	   THROW_EXCEPTION("MmulHelper::dot: Z array must be scalar !");
  1282	 }
  1283	
  1284	 const auto length = X->lengthOf();
  1285	
  1286	 if (Y->lengthOf() != length)
  1287	   THROW_EXCEPTION("MmulHelper::dot cuda: lengths of input vectors are different !");
  1288	
  1289	 if (Z == nullptr)
  1290	   Z = new NDArray(DataTypeUtils::pickPairwiseResultType(X->dataType(), Y->dataType()), X->getContext());
  1291	
  1292	 const LongType incx = X->strideAt(xLenDim);
  1293	 const LongType incy = Y->strideAt(yLenDim);
  1294	
  1295	 const auto xType = X->dataType();
  1296	 const auto yType = Y->dataType();
  1297	 const auto zType = Z->dataType();
  1298	
  1299	 if (!X->isActualOnDeviceSide()) X->syncToDevice();
  1300	 if (!Y->isActualOnDeviceSide()) Y->syncToDevice();
  1301	 if (!Z->isActualOnDeviceSide()) Z->syncToDevice();
  1302	
  1303	 cudaStream_t* stream = X->getContext()->getCudaStream();
  1304	
  1305	 dim3 dims = getMMulDims(length,DataTypeUtils::sizeOf(zType));
  1306	
  1307	 NDArray::prepareSpecialUse({Z}, {X, Y});
  1308	
  1309	
  1310	 BUILD_SINGLE_SELECTOR_THRICE(xType, usualDot,
  1311	                              (dims, stream, length, alpha, X->specialBuffer(), incx,
  1312	                               Y->specialBuffer(), incy, beta, Z->specialBuffer()),
  1313	                              SD_NUMERIC_TYPES);

codex
There’s one functional change outside the listed five: `mmulMxV` now casts mixed FP32/FP16/BF16 operands. That matters for the EOS regression context because logits can collapse if GEMV reads half data through a float kernel, so I’m reading that path with the same level of scrutiny as the MHA/decode fixes.

    } else {
      // Fallback: allocate new buffers
      std::vector<LongType> permBHSDtoBSHD = {0, 2, 1, 3};
      NDArray* pastKeyBSHD = pastKey->permute(permBHSDtoBSHD, false, false);
      NDArray* pastValueBSHD = pastValue->permute(permBHSDtoBSHD, false, false);

      std::vector<LongType> finalShape = {batch, totalSeqKV, numKvHeads, headDim};
      kFinal = new NDArray('c', finalShape, key->dataType(), block.launchContext());
      vFinal = new NDArray('c', finalShape, value->dataType(), block.launchContext());
      ownKVFinal = true;

      std::vector<LongType> pastSliceIdx = {0, batch, 0, pastSeq, 0, numKvHeads, 0, headDim};
      std::vector<LongType> curSliceIdx = {0, batch, pastSeq, totalSeqKV, 0, numKvHeads, 0, headDim};

      NDArray* kPastSlice = (*kFinal)(pastSliceIdx);
      NDArray* vPastSlice = (*vFinal)(pastSliceIdx);
      kPastSlice->assign(pastKeyBSHD);
      vPastSlice->assign(pastValueBSHD);
      delete kPastSlice;
      delete vPastSlice;

      NDArray* kCurSlice = (*kFinal)(curSliceIdx);
      NDArray* vCurSlice = (*vFinal)(curSliceIdx);
      kCurSlice->assign(kReshaped);
      vCurSlice->assign(vReshaped);
      delete kCurSlice;
      delete vCurSlice;

      delete pastKeyBSHD;
      delete pastValueBSHD;
    }
  } else {
    // No past - just use reshaped k/v directly
    kFinal = kReshaped;
    vFinal = vReshaped;
    ownKVFinal = false;  // Don't delete, kReshaped/vReshaped will be deleted later

    // Write current K/V as present outputs even without past
    // kReshaped is BSHD [batch, seqKV, numKvHeads, headDim], permute to BHSD for ONNX format
    std::vector<LongType> permBSHDtoBHSD = {0, 2, 1, 3};
    if (block.outputWidth() > 1) {
      auto presentKey = OUTPUT_VARIABLE(1);
      NDArray* kBHSD = kReshaped->permute(permBSHDtoBHSD, false, false);
      presentKey->assign(kBHSD);
      delete kBHSD;
    }
    if (block.outputWidth() > 2) {
      auto presentValue = OUTPUT_VARIABLE(2);
      NDArray* vBHSD = vReshaped->permute(permBSHDtoBHSD, false, false);
      presentValue->assign(vBHSD);
      delete vBHSD;
    }
  }
  
  // Setup FlashAttentionHelper config
  FlashAttentionHelper::Config config;
  config.scale = static_cast<float>(scale);
  config.isCausal = useCausalMask;
  config.dropout = 0.0f;
  config.numHeads = numHeads;
  config.numKvHeads = numKvHeads;
  
  // Output in BSHD format [batch, seqQ, numHeads, headDim]
  // Use AttentionWorkspace to reuse the buffer across invocations instead of
  // allocating a new GPU buffer every call (30 layers × every decode step).
  std::vector<LongType> outShape4d = {batch, seqQ, numHeads, headDim};
  auto workspace = AttentionWorkspace::getInstance();
  NDArray* attnOut4d = workspace->getBuffer("mha_attnOut4d", outShape4d, query->dataType(), block.launchContext());
  // Zero the workspace buffer to prevent stale data from a previous call leaking through.
  // AttentionWorkspace reuses buffers across calls without clearing them.  While
  // FlashAttention normally writes every output position, edge cases (maxKV==0, or
  // future kernel changes) could leave positions unwritten.  The cost of one memset
  // is negligible compared to the attention computation itself.
  attnOut4d->nullify();

  // Cast attention bias to query dtype if needed
  std::unique_ptr<NDArray> attnBiasCastOwner;
  if (attnBias != nullptr && attnBias->dataType() != query->dataType()) {
    attnBiasCastOwner.reset(attnBias->cast(query->dataType()));
    attnBias = attnBiasCastOwner.get();
  }
  
  // Diagnostic: dump Q checksum at the divergent position before FlashAttention
  if (sd::env_isDebugAndVerbose()) {
    qReshaped->syncToHost();
    kFinal->syncToHost();
    // Q is [batch, seqQ, numHeads, headDim] = [1, 17, 9, 64]
    // Dump head 2 at position 9 (where divergence occurs)
    if (seqQ > 9 && numHeads > 2) {
      sd_printf("MHA_DIAG: Q[0,9,2,0:4] = [%.6f, %.6f, %.6f, %.6f]\n",
                qReshaped->e<float>(0*seqQ*numHeads*headDim + 9*numHeads*headDim + 2*headDim + 0),
                qReshaped->e<float>(0*seqQ*numHeads*headDim + 9*numHeads*headDim + 2*headDim + 1),
                qReshaped->e<float>(0*seqQ*numHeads*headDim + 9*numHeads*headDim + 2*headDim + 2),
                qReshaped->e<float>(0*seqQ*numHeads*headDim + 9*numHeads*headDim + 2*headDim + 3));
      sd_printf("MHA_DIAG: K[0,9,0,0:4] = [%.6f, %.6f, %.6f, %.6f]\n",
                kFinal->e<float>(0*seqQ*numKvHeads*headDim + 9*numKvHeads*headDim + 0*headDim + 0),
                kFinal->e<float>(0*seqQ*numKvHeads*headDim + 9*numKvHeads*headDim + 0*headDim + 1),
                kFinal->e<float>(0*seqQ*numKvHeads*headDim + 9*numKvHeads*headDim + 0*headDim + 2),
                kFinal->e<float>(0*seqQ*numKvHeads*headDim + 9*numKvHeads*headDim + 0*headDim + 3));
      // Compute a simple checksum of all Q values
      double qSum = 0;
      for (LongType i = 0; i < std::min((LongType)100, qReshaped->lengthOf()); i++) {
        qSum += qReshaped->e<float>(i);
      }
      sd_printf("MHA_DIAG: Q checksum(first100) = %.8f, numHeads=%lld, numKvHeads=%lld, scale=%.8f\n",
                qSum, numHeads, numKvHeads, scale);
    }
  }

  // Call FlashAttentionHelper::forward with 4D tensors (BSHD format)
  FlashAttentionHelper::forward(qReshaped, kFinal, vFinal, attnOut4d, config,
                                nullptr, nullptr, nullptr,
                                block.launchContext(), attnBias);
  
  // Reshape output back to [batch, seqQ, hidden]
  // Use copyToNewBuff=false to create a view (no GPU allocation).
  std::vector<LongType> outShape3d = {batch, seqQ, hidden};
  NDArray* attnOutFinal = attnOut4d->reshape('c', outShape3d, false);
  output->assign(attnOutFinal);
  delete attnOutFinal;
  
  // Output present key/value if requested (in BHSD format for ONNX compatibility)
  // Skip if we already wrote directly to output buffers (ownKVFinal == false && using output view)
  if (block.outputWidth() > 1 && ownKVFinal) {
    auto presentKey = OUTPUT_VARIABLE(1);
    // kFinal is BSHD [batch, totalSeqKV, numKvHeads, headDim], permute to BHSD
    std::vector<LongType> permBSHDtoBHSD = {0, 2, 1, 3};
    NDArray* kBHSD = kFinal->permute(permBSHDtoBHSD, false, false);
    presentKey->assign(kBHSD);
    delete kBHSD;
  }
  if (block.outputWidth() > 2 && ownKVFinal) {
    auto presentValue = OUTPUT_VARIABLE(2);
    std::vector<LongType> permBSHDtoBHSD2 = {0, 2, 1, 3};
    NDArray* vBHSD = vFinal->permute(permBSHDtoBHSD2, false, false);
    presentValue->assign(vBHSD);
    delete vBHSD;
  }
  
  // Clean up
  if (ownKVFinal) {
    delete kFinal;
    delete vFinal;
  }
  delete qReshaped;
  delete kReshaped;
  delete vReshaped;
  // qContig/kContig/vContig are just aliases, not owned copies

  return sd::Status::OK;
}

DECLARE_TYPES(onnx_multi_head_attention) {
  getOpDescriptor()
      ->setAllowedInputTypes(0, {ALL_FLOATS})   // query
      ->setAllowedInputTypes(1, {ALL_FLOATS})   // key
      ->setAllowedInputTypes(2, {ALL_FLOATS})   // value
      ->setAllowedInputTypes(3, {ALL_FLOATS, ALL_INTS, BOOL})  // attn_bias (optional)
      ->setAllowedInputTypes(4, {ALL_FLOATS})   // past_key (optional)
      ->setAllowedInputTypes(5, {ALL_FLOATS})   // past_value (optional)
      ->setAllowedInputTypes(6, {ALL_INTS})     // cache_position (optional, INT64 scalar)
      ->setAllowedOutputTypes({ALL_FLOATS})
      ->addTraits(OP_TRAIT_ATTENTION | OP_TRAIT_FULLY_WRITING);
}

    // Use specialBuffer on CUDA (device pointer), buffer on CPU (host pointer)
#if defined(SD_CUDA)
    const void* cachePosPtr = cachePosInput->specialBuffer();
#else
    const void* cachePosPtr = cachePosInput->buffer();
#endif

    // Write into the ORIGINAL persistent KV cache buffers, not cast temporaries.
    // If pastKey was cast to a different dtype, origPastKey points to the real cache.
    helpers::kvInPlaceWrite(origPastKey, kReshaped, cachePosPtr, block.launchContext());
    helpers::kvInPlaceWrite(origPastValue, vReshaped, cachePosPtr, block.launchContext());

    // Use the ORIGINAL pastKey/pastValue for attention (permute BHSD→BSHD).
    // totalSeqKV = maxSeqLen: the causal mask handles token visibility,
    // so we pass the full buffer. Positions beyond cachePos+seqKV contain
    // zeros or stale data but are masked out by the attention mask.
    totalSeqKV = origPastKey->sizeAt(2);  // maxSeqLen

    std::vector<LongType> permBHSDtoBSHD = {0, 2, 1, 3};
    kFinal = origPastKey->permute(permBHSDtoBSHD, false, false);
    vFinal = origPastValue->permute(permBHSDtoBSHD, false, false);
    ownKVFinal = true;   // We own the permuted views (need delete)
    skipPresentOutput = true;  // No present output copy needed — past IS the cache
  } else if (pastKey != nullptr && pastValue != nullptr && !pastAlreadyConcat) {
    // Standard concat mode: concatenate past + current KV
    // Past is [batch, numKvHeads, pastSeq, headDim] (BHSD format from ONNX)
    // Need to permute to [batch, pastSeq, numKvHeads, headDim] (BSHD) for concat
    auto pastSeq = pastKey->sizeAt(2);
    totalSeqKV = pastSeq + seqKV;

    // Get output buffers for direct write
    auto presentKeyOut = block.outputWidth() > 1 ? OUTPUT_VARIABLE(1) : nullptr;
    auto presentValueOut = block.outputWidth() > 2 ? OUTPUT_VARIABLE(2) : nullptr;

    if (presentKeyOut != nullptr && presentValueOut != nullptr) {
      // Write directly to output buffers - they are already the correct size [batch, numKvHeads, totalSeqKV, headDim]
      // Permute output from BHSD to BSHD for use in attention
      std::vector<LongType> permBHSDtoBSHD = {0, 2, 1, 3};
      kFinal = presentKeyOut->permute(permBHSDtoBSHD, false, false);
      vFinal = presentValueOut->permute(permBHSDtoBSHD, false, false);
      ownKVFinal = false;

      // Copy past KV to [0:pastSeq] positions
      NDArray* pastKeyBSHD = pastKey->permute(permBHSDtoBSHD, false, false);
      NDArray* pastValueBSHD = pastValue->permute(permBHSDtoBSHD, false, false);

      std::vector<LongType> pastSliceIdx = {0, batch, 0, pastSeq, 0, numKvHeads, 0, headDim};
      NDArray* kPastSlice = (*kFinal)(pastSliceIdx);
      NDArray* vPastSlice = (*vFinal)(pastSliceIdx);
      kPastSlice->assign(pastKeyBSHD);
      vPastSlice->assign(pastValueBSHD);
      delete kPastSlice;
      delete vPastSlice;
      delete pastKeyBSHD;
      delete pastValueBSHD;

      // Write current K/V to [pastSeq:totalSeqKV] position
      std::vector<LongType> curSliceIdx = {0, batch, pastSeq, totalSeqKV, 0, numKvHeads, 0, headDim};
      NDArray* kCurSlice = (*kFinal)(curSliceIdx);
      NDArray* vCurSlice = (*vFinal)(curSliceIdx);

      if (kCurSlice->lengthOf() > 0 && kReshaped->lengthOf() > 0) {
        kCurSlice->applyTrueBroadcast(BroadcastOpsTuple::Assign(), kReshaped, kCurSlice, false);
        vCurSlice->applyTrueBroadcast(BroadcastOpsTuple::Assign(), vReshaped, vCurSlice, false);
      }

      delete kCurSlice;
      delete vCurSlice;
    } else {
      // Fallback: allocate new buffers
      std::vector<LongType> permBHSDtoBSHD = {0, 2, 1, 3};
      NDArray* pastKeyBSHD = pastKey->permute(permBHSDtoBSHD, false, false);
      NDArray* pastValueBSHD = pastValue->permute(permBHSDtoBSHD, false, false);

      std::vector<LongType> finalShape = {batch, totalSeqKV, numKvHeads, headDim};
      kFinal = new NDArray('c', finalShape, key->dataType(), block.launchContext());
      vFinal = new NDArray('c', finalShape, value->dataType(), block.launchContext());
      ownKVFinal = true;

      std::vector<LongType> pastSliceIdx = {0, batch, 0, pastSeq, 0, numKvHeads, 0, headDim};
      std::vector<LongType> curSliceIdx = {0, batch, pastSeq, totalSeqKV, 0, numKvHeads, 0, headDim};

      NDArray* kPastSlice = (*kFinal)(pastSliceIdx);
      NDArray* vPastSlice = (*vFinal)(pastSliceIdx);
      kPastSlice->assign(pastKeyBSHD);
      vPastSlice->assign(pastValueBSHD);
      delete kPastSlice;
      delete vPastSlice;

      NDArray* kCurSlice = (*kFinal)(curSliceIdx);
      NDArray* vCurSlice = (*vFinal)(curSliceIdx);
      kCurSlice->assign(kReshaped);
      vCurSlice->assign(vReshaped);
      delete kCurSlice;
      delete vCurSlice;

      delete pastKeyBSHD;
      delete pastValueBSHD;
    }
  } else {
    // No past - just use reshaped k/v directly
    kFinal = kReshaped;
    vFinal = vReshaped;
    ownKVFinal = false;  // Don't delete, kReshaped/vReshaped will be deleted later

    // Write current K/V as present outputs even without past
    // kReshaped is BSHD [batch, seqKV, numKvHeads, headDim], permute to BHSD for ONNX format
    std::vector<LongType> permBSHDtoBHSD = {0, 2, 1, 3};
    if (block.outputWidth() > 1) {
      auto presentKey = OUTPUT_VARIABLE(1);
      NDArray* kBHSD = kReshaped->permute(permBSHDtoBHSD, false, false);
      presentKey->assign(kBHSD);
      delete kBHSD;
    }
    if (block.outputWidth() > 2) {
      auto presentValue = OUTPUT_VARIABLE(2);
      NDArray* vBHSD = vReshaped->permute(permBSHDtoBHSD, false, false);
      presentValue->assign(vBHSD);
      delete vBHSD;
    }
  }
  
  // Setup FlashAttentionHelper config
  FlashAttentionHelper::Config config;
  config.scale = static_cast<float>(scale);
  config.isCausal = useCausalMask;
  config.dropout = 0.0f;
  config.numHeads = numHeads;
  config.numKvHeads = numKvHeads;
  
  // Cast attention bias to query dtype if needed
  std::unique_ptr<NDArray> attnBiasCastOwner;
  if (attnBias != nullptr && attnBias->dataType() != query->dataType()) {
    attnBiasCastOwner.reset(attnBias->cast(query->dataType()));
    attnBias = attnBiasCastOwner.get();
  }

  // Reshape output [batch, seqQ, hidden] → [batch, seqQ, numHeads, headDim] for attention kernel.
  // Output is plan-allocated C-contiguous, so reshape is a zero-copy view — the kernel
  // writes directly into the output buffer, eliminating the workspace nullify + assign copy
  // (60 CUDA ops removed from graph per step for 30-layer model).
  std::vector<LongType> outShape4d = {batch, seqQ, numHeads, headDim};
  NDArray* attnOut4d = output->reshape('c', outShape4d, false);
  bool directWrite = (attnOut4d->specialBuffer() == output->specialBuffer());

  // Call FlashAttentionHelper::forward with 4D tensors (BSHD format)
  FlashAttentionHelper::forward(qReshaped, kFinal, vFinal, attnOut4d, config,
                                nullptr, nullptr, nullptr,
                                block.launchContext(), attnBias);

  if (!directWrite) {
    // Reshape created a copy (non-contiguous output) — copy back.
    std::vector<LongType> outShape3d = {batch, seqQ, hidden};
    NDArray* attnOutFinal = attnOut4d->reshape('c', outShape3d, false);
    output->assign(attnOutFinal);
    delete attnOutFinal;
  }
  delete attnOut4d;
  
  // Output present key/value if requested (in BHSD format for ONNX compatibility)
  // Skip if we already wrote directly to output buffers (in-place KV or direct write mode)
  if (block.outputWidth() > 1 && ownKVFinal && !skipPresentOutput) {
    auto presentKey = OUTPUT_VARIABLE(1);
    // kFinal is BSHD [batch, totalSeqKV, numKvHeads, headDim], permute to BHSD
    std::vector<LongType> permBSHDtoBHSD = {0, 2, 1, 3};
    NDArray* kBHSD = kFinal->permute(permBSHDtoBHSD, false, false);
    presentKey->assign(kBHSD);
    delete kBHSD;
  }
  if (block.outputWidth() > 2 && ownKVFinal && !skipPresentOutput) {
    auto presentValue = OUTPUT_VARIABLE(2);
    std::vector<LongType> permBSHDtoBHSD2 = {0, 2, 1, 3};
    NDArray* vBHSD = vFinal->permute(permBSHDtoBHSD2, false, false);
    presentValue->assign(vBHSD);
    delete vBHSD;
  }
  
  // Clean up
  if (ownKVFinal) {
    delete kFinal;
    delete vFinal;
  }
  delete qReshaped;
  delete kReshaped;
  delete vReshaped;
  // qContig/kContig/vContig are just aliases, not owned copies

  delete keyCast;
  delete valueCast;
  delete pastKeyCast;

    57	
    58	NDArray* AttentionWorkspace::getBuffer(const std::string& key,
    59	                                        const std::vector<LongType>& shape,
    60	                                        DataType dtype,
    61	                                        LaunchContext* context) {
    62	  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    63	
    64	  // Calculate required capacity
    65	  LongType requiredElements = 1;
    66	  for (auto dim : shape) {
    67	    requiredElements *= dim;
    68	  }
    69	  size_t requiredBytes = requiredElements * DataTypeUtils::sizeOf(dtype);
    70	
    71	  auto it = buffers_.find(key);
    72	  if (it != buffers_.end()) {
    73	    auto& existing = it->second;
    74	
    75	    // Check if existing buffer is compatible
    76	    if (existing.buffer->dataType() == dtype && existing.capacity >= requiredElements) {
    77	      // Check actual NDArray shape, not tracked currentShape — external reshapei can desync
    78	      auto* actualShapeVec = existing.buffer->getShapeAsVector();
    79	      bool shapeMatches = (*actualShapeVec == shape);
    80	      delete actualShapeVec;
    81	
    82	      if (shapeMatches) {
    83	        existing.lastUsed = ++accessCounter_;
    84	        // Return view if active (element count differs from buffer), otherwise return buffer
    85	        if (existing.view) {
    86	          return existing.view.get();
    87	        }
    88	        return existing.buffer.get();
    89	      }
    90	
    91	      // Shape mismatch — evict old buffer and reallocate.
    92	      // Previously this tried to reshape/reuse the buffer, but that breaks CUDA graphs:
    93	      // the graph records kernel launches with the old shape, and replay with reshaped
    94	      // buffers causes shape mismatches and crashes. Reallocation ensures fresh buffers
    95	      // with correct shape info are used.
    96	      currentMemory_ -= existing.capacity * DataTypeUtils::sizeOf(existing.buffer->dataType());
    97	      buffers_.erase(it);
    98	    } else {
    99	      // Buffer exists but wrong type or too small - need to reallocate
   100	      currentMemory_ -= existing.capacity * DataTypeUtils::sizeOf(existing.buffer->dataType());
   101	    }
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  // Evict old buffers if needed
   105	  evictIfNeeded(requiredBytes);
   106	
   107	  // Allocate new buffer - need mutable copy since NDArray constructor takes non-const ref
   108	  std::vector<LongType> mutableShape(shape);
   109	  auto buffer = std::make_unique<NDArray>('c', mutableShape, dtype, context);
   110	
   111	  BufferEntry entry;
   112	  entry.buffer = std::move(buffer);
   113	  entry.currentShape = shape;
   114	  entry.capacity = requiredElements;
   115	  entry.lastUsed = ++accessCounter_;
   116	
   117	  currentMemory_ += requiredBytes;
   118	  buffers_[key] = std::move(entry);
   119	
   120	  return buffers_[key].buffer.get();

   249	
   250	void autoregressiveDecodeCuda(
   251	    NDArray* prefillEmbeddings,
   252	    NDArray* embeddingTable,
   253	    NDArray* inputIds,
   254	    NDArray* attentionMask,
   255	    NDArray* positionIds,
   256	    NDArray** staticKvBuffers,
   257	    int numKvPairs,

   262	    int prefillSeqLen,
   263	    const std::vector<int>& stopTokenIds,
   264	    double temperature,
   265	    int topK,
   266	    double topP,
   267	    LaunchContext* context,
   268	    AutoregressiveDecodeConfig* config) {
   269	
   270	    auto stream = context->getCudaStream();

   272	    // Initialize outputs
   273	    LongType zero = 0;

   276	    tokenCount->assign(zero);
   277	    timingInfo->assign(zeroF);

   280	    REQUIRE_TRUE(config != nullptr && config->planHandle != nullptr, 0,
   281	                 "autoregressive_decode: no plan handle provided. "
   282	                 "The Java side MUST pass a compiled NativeDynamicShapePlan via config->planHandle. "
   283	                 "config=%p planHandle=%p",
   284	                 config, config ? config->planHandle : nullptr);

   286	    auto plan = config->planHandle;

   290	                  "embExtIdx=%d maskExtIdx=%d posExtIdx=%d idsExtIdx=%d causalExtIdx=%d "
   291	                  "attnReformatExtIdx=%d numKvPairs=%d logitsOutIdx=%d\n",
   292	                  plan, maxNewTokens, prefillSeqLen,
   293	                  config->embeddingsExtIdx, config->maskExtIdx, config->posIdsExtIdx,
   294	                  config->inputIdsExtIdx, config->causalMaskExtIdx,
   295	                  config->attnMaskReformatExtIdx, numKvPairs, config->logitsOutputIdx);
   296	    }
   297	
   298	    // ── Timing ──
   299	    std::vector<double> stepTimesMs;
   300	    stepTimesMs.reserve(maxNewTokens);

   302	
   303	    // ── Internal state ──

   306	    auto vocabSize = embeddingTable->sizeAt(0);

   314	        maxKvLen = attentionMask->sizeAt(-1);

    41	/**
    42	 * CPU: look up a single row from the embedding table.
    43	 * Copies embeddingTable[tokenId, :] into outputEmbed [1, 1, hidden].
    44	 */
    45	template <typename T>
    46	static void embedLookupCpu(const void* vEmbTable, void* vOutput,
    47	                           LongType tokenId, LongType hidden, LongType tableRowStride) {
    48	    auto embTable = reinterpret_cast<const T*>(vEmbTable);
    49	    auto output = reinterpret_cast<T*>(vOutput);
    50	    LongType baseOffset = tokenId * tableRowStride;
    51	    for (LongType i = 0; i < hidden; i++) {
    52	        output[i] = embTable[baseOffset + i];
    53	    }
    54	}

    59	template <typename T>

    62	    if (position < maxKvLen) {

    65	}

    76	}

    81	template <typename T>
    82	static void buildInitialMaskCpu(void* vMask, LongType prefillSeqLen, LongType maxKvLen) {
    83	    auto mask = reinterpret_cast<T*>(vMask);
    84	    for (LongType i = 0; i < prefillSeqLen && i < maxKvLen; i++) {
    85	        mask[i] = static_cast<T>(1);

    89	/**
    90	 * CPU: argmax over a float/half row [vocabSize].
    91	 * Writes the index to output[0] as INT64.
    92	 */
    93	template <typename T>
    94	static void argmaxCpu(const void* vLogits, void* vOutput, LongType vocabSize) {
    95	    auto logits = reinterpret_cast<const T*>(vLogits);
    96	    auto output = reinterpret_cast<LongType*>(vOutput);
    97	    if (vocabSize <= 0) {
    98	        output[0] = 0;
    99	        return;
   100	    }
   101	    T maxVal = logits[0];
   102	    LongType maxIdx = 0;
   103	    for (LongType i = 1; i < vocabSize; i++) {
   104	        if (logits[i] > maxVal) {
   105	            maxVal = logits[i];
   106	            maxIdx = i;
   107	        }
   108	    }
   109	    output[0] = maxIdx;
   110	}

    36	/**
    37	 * Configuration for decoder model input/output variable names.
    38	 *
    39	 * <p>Eliminates hardcoded variable name assumptions throughout the decode pipeline.
    40	 * All components (GenerationPipeline, DraftModelSpeculator, FrozenDecodeStep)
    41	 * use this config instead of comparing against string literals like
    42	 * "inputs_embeds", "attention_mask", etc.</p>
    43	 *
    44	 * <p>Provides a {@link #discover(SameDiff)} factory that auto-discovers names from
    45	 * the SameDiff graph, and a builder for explicit configuration.</p>
    46	 *
    47	 * @see DraftModelSpeculator
    48	 * @see FrozenDecodeStep
    49	 * @see GenerationPipeline
    50	 */
    51	@Slf4j
    52	@Getter
    53	@Builder
    54	public class ModelIOConfig {
    55	
    56	    /** Mask fill value — use -65504 (torch.finfo(torch.float16).min) to avoid
    57	     *  float16 overflow in SDPA kernels. The old value (-3.4e38, float32 min) overflowed
    58	     *  to -inf in float16 and caused numerical instability with large padded sequences. */
    59	    public static final float MASK_FILL = -65504.0f;
    60	
    61	    /**
    62	     * Holds lists of present key and value output names from a decoder model.
    63	     */
    64	    public static class KVCacheNames {
    65	        public final List<String> keyNames;
    66	        public final List<String> valueNames;
    67	
    68	        public KVCacheNames(List<String> keyNames, List<String> valueNames) {
    69	            this.keyNames = keyNames;
    70	            this.valueNames = valueNames;
    71	        }
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    /**
    75	     * Find the logits output variable name from a decoder model.
    76	     */
    77	    public static String findLogitsOutputName(SameDiff decoder) {
    78	        for (String outputName : decoder.outputs()) {
    79	            if (outputName.contains("logit") || outputName.equals("logits")) {
    80	                return outputName;
    81	            }
    82	        }
    83	        if (!decoder.outputs().isEmpty()) {
    84	            return decoder.outputs().get(0);
    85	        }
    86	        return null;
    87	    }
    88	
    89	    /**
    90	     * Find the KV cache output names (present key/value) from a decoder model.
    91	     */
    92	    public static KVCacheNames findKVCacheOutputNames(SameDiff decoder) {
    93	        List<String> presentKeyNames = new ArrayList<>();
    94	        List<String> presentValueNames = new ArrayList<>();
    95	
    96	        for (String outputName : decoder.outputs()) {
    97	            if (outputName.contains("present") && outputName.contains("key")) {
    98	                presentKeyNames.add(outputName);
    99	            } else if (outputName.contains("present") && outputName.contains("value")) {
   100	                presentValueNames.add(outputName);
   101	            }
   102	        }
   103	
   104	        Collections.sort(presentKeyNames);
   105	        Collections.sort(presentValueNames);
   106	
   107	        return new KVCacheNames(presentKeyNames, presentValueNames);
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    /**
   111	     * Find KV cache INPUT names from a decoder model.
   112	     *
   113	     * <p>GGUF models with in-graph KV cache have inputs like:
   114	     * {@code past_key_values.0.key}, {@code past_key_values.0.value}, etc.
   115	     * These are static buffers that the attention op writes to in-place.</p>
   116	     *
   117	     * @param decoder the SameDiff decoder model
   118	     * @return KVCacheNames with input key/value names, or null if none found
   119	     */
   120	    public static KVCacheNames findKVCacheInputNames(SameDiff decoder) {
   121	        List<String> keyInputNames = new ArrayList<>();
   122	        List<String> valueInputNames = new ArrayList<>();
   123	
   124	        for (String inputName : decoder.inputs()) {
   125	            if (!inputName.contains("past_key_values")) continue;
   126	            // Match suffix: ".key" or ".value" — not substring, since "past_key_values" itself contains "key"
   127	            if (inputName.endsWith(".key")) {
   128	                keyInputNames.add(inputName);
   129	            } else if (inputName.endsWith(".value")) {
   130	                valueInputNames.add(inputName);
   131	            }
   132	        }
   133	
   134	        Collections.sort(keyInputNames);
   135	        Collections.sort(valueInputNames);
   136	
   137	        if (keyInputNames.isEmpty() && valueInputNames.isEmpty()) {
   138	            return null;
   139	        }
   140	        return new KVCacheNames(keyInputNames, valueInputNames);
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    /**
   144	     * Check whether a decoder uses in-graph KV cache (GGUF pattern).
   145	     *
   146	     * <p>In-graph KV cache means the model has KV cache INPUTS (past_key_values.*.key/value)
   147	     * but NO present KV outputs. The attention op updates the cache buffers in-place
   148	     * during execution. This differs from ONNX models which have present_* outputs
   149	     * that must be scattered back externally.</p>
   150	     *
   151	     * @param decoder the SameDiff decoder model
   152	     * @return true if the model uses in-graph KV cache
   153	     */
   154	    public static boolean isInGraphKvCache(SameDiff decoder) {
   155	        KVCacheNames inputKv = findKVCacheInputNames(decoder);
   156	        if (inputKv == null || inputKv.keyNames.isEmpty()) {
   157	            return false;
   158	        }
   159	        // In-graph KV cache: has KV cache inputs but no present outputs
   160	        KVCacheNames outputKv = findKVCacheOutputNames(decoder);
   161	        return outputKv.keyNames.isEmpty();
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    /**
   165	     * Check whether a decoder has any form of KV cache support.
   166	     *
   167	     * <p>Returns true for either:</p>
   168	     * <ul>
   169	     *   <li>ONNX models with present_* outputs (external KV scatter)</li>
   170	     *   <li>GGUF models with past_key_values.* inputs (in-graph KV cache)</li>
   171	     * </ul>
   172	     */
   173	    public static boolean hasKvCache(SameDiff decoder) {
   174	        KVCacheNames outputKv = findKVCacheOutputNames(decoder);
   175	        if (outputKv != null && !outputKv.keyNames.isEmpty()) {
   176	            return true;
   177	        }
   178	        return isInGraphKvCache(decoder);
   179	    }

   183	     *
   184	     * For prefill (currentSeqLen &gt; 1): upper-triangular mask filled with MASK_FILL

   193	    public static INDArray buildCausalMask(long currentSeqLen, long totalSeqLen) {

   197	    /**
   198	     * Build a batched causal attention mask for the decoder (FLOAT dtype).
   199	     *
   200	     * @param batchSize number of sequences in the batch
   201	     * @param currentSeqLen number of query tokens in this step

   203	     * @return INDArray of shape [batchSize, 1, currentSeqLen, totalSeqLen] with FLOAT dtype
   204	     */
   205	    public static INDArray buildCausalMask(long batchSize, long currentSeqLen, long totalSeqLen) {
   206	        if (currentSeqLen == 1) {
   207	            return Nd4j.zeros(DataType.FLOAT, batchSize, 1, 1, totalSeqLen);
   208	        }
   209	
   210	        long pastSeqLen = totalSeqLen - currentSeqLen;
   211	        int Q = (int) currentSeqLen;
   212	        int K = (int) totalSeqLen;
   213	        float[] data = new float[Q * K];
   214	
   215	        for (int q = 0; q < Q; q++) {
   216	            int lastVisibleK = (int) pastSeqLen + q;
   217	            int rowOffset = q * K;
   218	            for (int k = lastVisibleK + 1; k < K; k++) {
   219	                data[rowOffset + k] = MASK_FILL;
   220	            }

   500	     *
   501	     * <p>Inspects the model's input and output variable names to find:
   502	     * <ul>
   503	     *   <li>Input embeddings: first input containing "embed"</li>
   504	     *   <li>Input IDs: first input containing "input_id"</li>
   505	     *   <li>Attention mask: first input containing "attention_mask"</li>
   506	     *   <li>Causal mask: first input containing "causal_mask"</li>
   507	     *   <li>Position IDs: first input containing "position_id"</li>
   508	     *   <li>KV cache prefix: detected from first input starting with common KV prefixes</li>
   509	     *   <li>Logits output: via {@link #findLogitsOutputName(SameDiff)}</li>
   510	     *   <li>KV cache outputs: via {@link #findKVCacheOutputNames(SameDiff)}</li>
   511	     * </ul></p>
   512	     *
   513	     * @param model the SameDiff model to inspect
   514	     * @return a ModelIOConfig with discovered names (with sensible defaults for any not found)
   515	     */
   516	    public static ModelIOConfig discover(SameDiff model) {

   519	        String embedName = null;
   520	        String idsName = null;
   521	        String maskName = null;

   525	        String cachePosName = null;

   529	        String encoderMaskName = null;

   534	                encoderHiddenName = input;
   535	            } else if (encoderMaskName == null && input.contains("encoder") && input.contains("attention_mask")) {
   536	                encoderMaskName = input;
   537	            } else if (embedName == null && input.contains("embed")) {
   538	                embedName = input;
   539	            } else if (idsName == null && input.contains("input_id")) {
   540	                idsName = input;

   548	                posOffsetName = input;

   557	                } else if (input.startsWith("past_key_value.")) {
   558	                    kvPrefix = "past_key_value.";
   559	                } else if (input.startsWith("pkv.")) {
   560	                    kvPrefix = "pkv.";
   561	                }
   562	            }

   565	        // If input_ids exists as a variable but wasn't found in inputs(),

   568	        if (idsName == null && model.hasVariable("input_ids")) {

   632	        return config;
