    20	
    21	package org.eclipse.deeplearning4j.nd4j.linalg.ops;
    22	
    23	import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
    24	import org.nd4j.linalg.api.buffer.DataType;
    25	import org.nd4j.linalg.api.ndarray.INDArray;
    26	import org.nd4j.linalg.api.ops.impl.transforms.custom.KvScatter;
    27	import org.nd4j.linalg.api.ops.impl.transforms.custom.OnnxMultiHeadAttention;
    28	import org.nd4j.linalg.factory.Nd4j;
    29	import org.nd4j.linalg.indexing.NDArrayIndex;
    30	
    31	import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
    32	
    33	/**
    34	 * Tests for multi-step MHA decode with KV cache, verifying that KV values
    35	 * don't grow unboundedly. This directly investigates the root cause of
    36	 * degenerate VLM output (exploding attention scores).
    37	 *
    38	 * The decode pattern simulates what the decode pipeline does:
    39	 * 1. Prefill: run MHA with Q/K/V from embeddings (seqLen > 1)
    40	 * 2. Pad KV to static size (maxKvLen)
    41	 * 3. Decode steps: run MHA with seqLen=1, scatter new KV entry into static buffer
    42	 *
    43	 * The test verifies that after N decode steps, KV values remain bounded.
    44	 */
    45	public class TestMhaMultiStepKvStability {
    46	
    47	    private static final int NUM_HEADS = 4;
    48	    private static final int HEAD_DIM = 16;
    49	    private static final int HIDDEN = NUM_HEADS * HEAD_DIM;
    50	
    51	    /**
    52	     * Test the VIEW-BASED decode path (no freeze / no DSP).
    53	     * Past KV is a growing view [0:cachePos] of the static buffer.
    54	     * MHA concatenates past + new, KvScatter writes last position back.
    55	     */
    56	    @Test
    57	    public void testViewBasedDecodeKvStability() {
    58	        int B = 1;
    59	        int prefillLen = 10;
    60	        int maxKvLen = prefillLen + 50;
    61	        int decodeSteps = 20;
    62	
    63	        // Prefill: generate initial KV from random Q/K/V
    64	        INDArray prefillQ = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, prefillLen, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
    65	        INDArray prefillK = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, prefillLen, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
    66	        INDArray prefillV = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, prefillLen, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
    67	
    68	        INDArray[] prefillResult = Nd4j.exec(new OnnxMultiHeadAttention(
    69	                prefillQ, prefillK, prefillV, null, null, null,
    70	                NUM_HEADS, 0.0, false));
    71	
    72	        INDArray presentKey = prefillResult[1]; // [B, numHeads, prefillLen, headDim]
    73	        INDArray presentValue = prefillResult[2];
    74	
    75	        // Pad to static size
    76	        INDArray staticKey = Nd4j.zeros(DataType.FLOAT, B, NUM_HEADS, maxKvLen, HEAD_DIM);
    77	        INDArray staticValue = Nd4j.zeros(DataType.FLOAT, B, NUM_HEADS, maxKvLen, HEAD_DIM);
    78	        staticKey.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
    79	                NDArrayIndex.interval(0, prefillLen), NDArrayIndex.all()).assign(presentKey);
    80	        staticValue.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
    81	                NDArrayIndex.interval(0, prefillLen), NDArrayIndex.all()).assign(presentValue);
    82	
    83	        long cachePos = prefillLen;
    84	        double prevKvMax = presentKey.amaxNumber().doubleValue();
    85	        System.out.println("Prefill KV absMax: " + prevKvMax);
    86	
    87	        // Decode steps with view-based KV
    88	        for (int step = 0; step < decodeSteps; step++) {
    89	            // New token embedding (simulated)
    90	            INDArray newQ = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, 1, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
    91	            INDArray newK = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, 1, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
    92	            INDArray newV = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, 1, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
    93	
    94	            // View-based: pass [0:cachePos] slice as past KV
    95	            INDArray pastKeyView = staticKey.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
    96	                    NDArrayIndex.interval(0, cachePos), NDArrayIndex.all());
    97	            INDArray pastValueView = staticValue.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
    98	                    NDArrayIndex.interval(0, cachePos), NDArrayIndex.all());
    99	
   100	            INDArray[] decodeResult = Nd4j.exec(new OnnxMultiHeadAttention(
   101	                    newQ, newK, newV, null, pastKeyView, pastValueView,
   102	                    NUM_HEADS, 0.0, false));
   103	
   104	            INDArray decodePresentKey = decodeResult[1]; // [B, numHeads, cachePos+1, headDim]
   105	            INDArray decodePresentValue = decodeResult[2];
   106	
   107	            // Verify present output shapes
   108	            assertEquals(cachePos + 1, decodePresentKey.size(2),
   109	                    "Present key seq len at step " + step);
   110	
   111	            // Scatter last position from present into static buffer
   112	            Nd4j.exec(new KvScatter(staticKey, decodePresentKey, cachePos));
   113	            Nd4j.exec(new KvScatter(staticValue, decodePresentValue, cachePos));
   114	
   115	            // Check KV value magnitude
   116	            double kvMax = staticKey.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
   117	                    NDArrayIndex.point(cachePos), NDArrayIndex.all()).amaxNumber().doubleValue();
   118	
   119	            System.out.printf("Step %d: cachePos=%d, newKvMax=%.4f, prevKvMax=%.4f%n",
   120	                    step, cachePos, kvMax, prevKvMax);
   121	
   122	            // KV values should NOT grow unboundedly
   123	            // Allow 5x the initial max as tolerance (some growth is OK for random inputs)
   124	            assertTrue(kvMax < prevKvMax * 5 + 1.0,
   125	                    "KV values growing unboundedly at step " + step +
   126	                            ": kvMax=" + kvMax + " vs initial=" + prevKvMax);
   127	
   128	            cachePos++;
   129	        }
   130	
   131	        // Final check: KV magnitude should be in same order of magnitude as initial
   132	        double finalKvMax = staticKey.amaxNumber().doubleValue();
   133	        System.out.println("Final KV absMax: " + finalKvMax + " vs initial: " + prevKvMax);
   134	        assertTrue(finalKvMax < prevKvMax * 10 + 1.0,
   135	                "KV values exploded: final=" + finalKvMax + " vs initial=" + prevKvMax);
   136	    }

   138	    /**
   139	     * Test that the MHA concat properly assembles present_key = concat(past, new).
   140	     * Verify the new entry matches the input K (after reshape/permute).
   141	     */
   142	    @Test
   143	    public void testMhaConcatCorrectness() {
   144	        int B = 1, pastSeq = 5;
   145	
   146	        // Create known past KV
   147	        INDArray pastKey = Nd4j.linspace(1, B * NUM_HEADS * pastSeq * HEAD_DIM, B * NUM_HEADS * pastSeq * HEAD_DIM)
   148	                .reshape(B, NUM_HEADS, pastSeq, HEAD_DIM).castTo(DataType.FLOAT);
   149	        INDArray pastValue = pastKey.mul(0.5);
   150	
   151	        // New token with known values
   152	        INDArray newQ = Nd4j.ones(DataType.FLOAT, B, 1, HIDDEN);
   153	        INDArray newK = Nd4j.ones(DataType.FLOAT, B, 1, HIDDEN).muli(99.0);
   154	        INDArray newV = Nd4j.ones(DataType.FLOAT, B, 1, HIDDEN).muli(-99.0);
   155	
   156	        INDArray[] result = Nd4j.exec(new OnnxMultiHeadAttention(
   157	                newQ, newK, newV, null, pastKey, pastValue,
   158	                NUM_HEADS, 0.0, false));
   159	
   160	        INDArray presentKeyOut = result[1]; // [B, numHeads, pastSeq+1, headDim]
   161	        INDArray presentValueOut = result[2];
   162	
   163	        assertEquals(pastSeq + 1, presentKeyOut.size(2), "Present key should have pastSeq+1 positions");
   164	        assertEquals(pastSeq + 1, presentValueOut.size(2), "Present value should have pastSeq+1 positions");
   165	
   166	        // Verify past portion preserved
   167	        INDArray pastPortion = presentKeyOut.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
   168	                NDArrayIndex.interval(0, pastSeq), NDArrayIndex.all());
   169	        double pastDiff = pastPortion.sub(pastKey).amaxNumber().doubleValue();
   170	        assertTrue(pastDiff < 1e-4,
   171	                "Past KV portion should be preserved, diff=" + pastDiff);
   172	
   173	        // Verify new entry is at position pastSeq
   174	        INDArray newEntry = presentKeyOut.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
   175	                NDArrayIndex.point(pastSeq), NDArrayIndex.all());
   176	        // New K was [B, 1, HIDDEN] = 99.0 everywhere
   177	        // After reshape [B, 1, numHeads, headDim] and permute to BHSD, each entry = 99.0
   178	        double newMax = newEntry.amaxNumber().doubleValue();
   179	        double newMin = newEntry.aminNumber().doubleValue();
   180	        assertEquals(99.0, newMax, 0.01, "New key entry max should be 99.0");
   181	        assertEquals(99.0, newMin, 0.01, "New key entry min should be 99.0 (uniform)");
   182	    }

   184	    /**
   185	     * Test KvScatter correctness: verify it overwrites (not accumulates).
   186	     */
   187	    @Test
   188	    public void testKvScatterOverwrites() {
   189	        int B = 1, maxSeq = 10;
   190	        INDArray staticBuf = Nd4j.zeros(DataType.FLOAT, B, NUM_HEADS, maxSeq, HEAD_DIM);
   191	        INDArray present = Nd4j.ones(DataType.FLOAT, B, NUM_HEADS, 3, HEAD_DIM).muli(42.0);
   192	
   193	        // Scatter at position 5 (should write present's last position = position 2)
   194	        Nd4j.exec(new KvScatter(staticBuf, present, 5));
   195	
   196	        // Position 5 should have value 42.0
   197	        INDArray pos5 = staticBuf.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
   198	                NDArrayIndex.point(5), NDArrayIndex.all());
   199	        assertEquals(42.0, pos5.getDouble(0), 0.01);
   200	
   201	        // Now scatter different value at same position
   202	        INDArray present2 = Nd4j.ones(DataType.FLOAT, B, NUM_HEADS, 1, HEAD_DIM).muli(7.0);
   203	        Nd4j.exec(new KvScatter(staticBuf, present2, 5));
   204	
   205	        // Position 5 should now be 7.0 (overwritten, not 42+7=49)
   206	        INDArray pos5After = staticBuf.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
   207	                NDArrayIndex.point(5), NDArrayIndex.all());
   208	        assertEquals(7.0, pos5After.getDouble(0), 0.01,
   209	                "KvScatter should overwrite, not accumulate");
   210	    }

   212	    /**
   213	     * Test padded-mode decode: full static buffer passed as past KV.
   214	     * This is the path used with DSP/CUDA graphs.
   215	     * The key question: does attending to zero-padded positions cause issues?
   216	     */
   217	    @Test
   218	    public void testPaddedModeDecodeStability() {
   219	        int B = 1;
   220	        int prefillLen = 5;
   221	        int maxKvLen = 20;
   222	        int decodeSteps = 10;
   223	
   224	        // Prefill
   225	        INDArray prefillQ = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, prefillLen, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
   226	        INDArray prefillK = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, prefillLen, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
   227	        INDArray prefillV = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, prefillLen, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
   228	
   229	        INDArray[] prefillResult = Nd4j.exec(new OnnxMultiHeadAttention(
   230	                prefillQ, prefillK, prefillV, null, null, null,
   231	                NUM_HEADS, 0.0, false));
   232	
   233	        // Pad to maxKvLen
   234	        INDArray staticKey = Nd4j.zeros(DataType.FLOAT, B, NUM_HEADS, maxKvLen, HEAD_DIM);
   235	        INDArray staticValue = Nd4j.zeros(DataType.FLOAT, B, NUM_HEADS, maxKvLen, HEAD_DIM);
   236	        staticKey.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
   237	                NDArrayIndex.interval(0, prefillLen), NDArrayIndex.all()).assign(prefillResult[1]);
   238	        staticValue.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
   239	                NDArrayIndex.interval(0, prefillLen), NDArrayIndex.all()).assign(prefillResult[2]);
   240	
   241	        long cachePos = prefillLen;
   242	        double initialKvMax = prefillResult[1].amaxNumber().doubleValue();
   243	
   244	        // Decode with FULL padded buffer as past KV (simulates DSP/frozen path)
   245	        for (int step = 0; step < decodeSteps; step++) {
   246	            INDArray newQ = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, 1, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
   247	            INDArray newK = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, 1, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
   248	            INDArray newV = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, 1, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
   249	
   250	            // Build attention bias: mask out future + unused positions
   251	            // [B, numHeads, 1, maxKvLen+1] — MHA doesn't broadcast head dim
   252	            long totalKv = maxKvLen + 1; // concat will make this
   253	            INDArray attnBias = Nd4j.zeros(DataType.FLOAT, B, NUM_HEADS, 1, totalKv);
   254	            // Mask positions after cachePos (zero padding) and the concat position
   255	            // For positions cachePos+1 to maxKvLen-1, fill with -3.4e38
   256	            float maskFill = -3.4028235e+38f;
   257	            for (long p = cachePos + 1; p < maxKvLen; p++) {
   258	                for (int h = 0; h < NUM_HEADS; h++) {
   259	                    attnBias.putScalar(new long[]{0, h, 0, p}, maskFill);
   260	                }
   261	            }
   262	            // Last position (the concat'd new token at index maxKvLen) should be visible
   263	            // so leave it as 0
   264	
   265	            INDArray[] decodeResult = Nd4j.exec(new OnnxMultiHeadAttention(
   266	                    newQ, newK, newV, attnBias, staticKey, staticValue,
   267	                    NUM_HEADS, 0.0, false));
   268	
   269	            INDArray decodePresentKey = decodeResult[1]; // [B, numHeads, maxKvLen+1, headDim]
   270	            assertEquals(maxKvLen + 1, decodePresentKey.size(2),
   271	                    "Padded present key should be maxKvLen + 1");
   272	
   273	            // Scatter: write last position into static buffer at cachePos
   274	            Nd4j.exec(new KvScatter(staticKey, decodePresentKey, cachePos));
   275	            Nd4j.exec(new KvScatter(staticValue, decodeResult[2], cachePos));
   276	
   277	            double kvMax = staticKey.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
   278	                    NDArrayIndex.point(cachePos), NDArrayIndex.all()).amaxNumber().doubleValue();
   279	
   280	            System.out.printf("Padded step %d: cachePos=%d, newKvMax=%.4f%n", step, cachePos, kvMax);
   281	
   282	            // KV values should remain bounded
   283	            assertTrue(kvMax < initialKvMax * 10 + 1.0,
   284	                    "Padded mode: KV values growing at step " + step +
   285	                            ": kvMax=" + kvMax + " vs initial=" + initialKvMax);
   286	
   287	            cachePos++;
   288	        }
   289	    }

   291	    /**
   292	     * Test that MHA without any past KV produces valid output.
   293	     */
   294	    @Test
   295	    public void testMhaBasicNoPast() {
   296	        int B = 1, seqLen = 5;
   297	
   298	        INDArray q = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, seqLen, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
   299	        INDArray k = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, seqLen, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
   300	        INDArray v = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, seqLen, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
   301	
   302	        INDArray[] result = Nd4j.exec(new OnnxMultiHeadAttention(
   303	                q, k, v, null, null, null,
   304	                NUM_HEADS, 0.0, false));
   305	
   306	        assertEquals(3, result.length);
   307	        assertArrayEquals(new long[]{B, seqLen, HIDDEN}, result[0].shape(), "Output shape");
   308	        assertArrayEquals(new long[]{B, NUM_HEADS, seqLen, HEAD_DIM}, result[1].shape(), "Present key shape");
   309	        assertArrayEquals(new long[]{B, NUM_HEADS, seqLen, HEAD_DIM}, result[2].shape(), "Present value shape");
   310	
   311	        assertFalse(result[0].isNaN().any(), "Output NaN");
   312	        assertFalse(result[0].isInfinite().any(), "Output Inf");
   313	    }

   315	    /**
   316	     * Test with causal masking enabled.
   317	     */
   318	    @Test
   319	    public void testMhaCausalMask() {
   320	        int B = 1, seqLen = 4;
   321	
   322	        INDArray q = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, seqLen, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
   323	        INDArray k = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, seqLen, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
   324	        INDArray v = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, seqLen, HIDDEN).muli(0.1);
   325	
   326	        // Without causal
   327	        INDArray[] noCausal = Nd4j.exec(new OnnxMultiHeadAttention(
   328	                q, k, v, null, null, null,
   329	                NUM_HEADS, 0.0, false));
   330	
   331	        // With causal
   332	        INDArray[] withCausal = Nd4j.exec(new OnnxMultiHeadAttention(
   333	                q, k, v, null, null, null,
   334	                NUM_HEADS, 0.0, true));
   335	
   336	        // Results should differ (causal mask restricts attention)
   337	        double diff = noCausal[0].sub(withCausal[0]).amaxNumber().doubleValue();
   338	        assertTrue(diff > 1e-6,
   339	                "Causal mask should change output, but diff was " + diff);
   340	
   341	        assertFalse(withCausal[0].isNaN().any());
   342	    }

   344	    /**
   345	     * Test GQA (Grouped Query Attention) where K/V have fewer heads than Q.
   346	     */
   347	    @Test
   348	    public void testMhaGqa() {
   349	        int B = 1, seqLen = 4;
   350	        int numQHeads = 8;
   351	        int numKvHeads = 2; // GQA: 8 Q heads, 2 KV heads (4:1 ratio)
   352	        int qHidden = numQHeads * HEAD_DIM;
   353	        int kvHidden = numKvHeads * HEAD_DIM;
   354	
   355	        INDArray q = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, seqLen, qHidden).muli(0.1);
   356	        INDArray k = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, seqLen, kvHidden).muli(0.1);
   357	        INDArray v = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, B, seqLen, kvHidden).muli(0.1);
   358	
   359	        INDArray[] result = Nd4j.exec(new OnnxMultiHeadAttention(
   360	                q, k, v, null, null, null,
   361	                numQHeads, 0.0, false));
   362	
   363	        assertArrayEquals(new long[]{B, seqLen, qHidden}, result[0].shape(), "GQA output shape");
   364	        assertArrayEquals(new long[]{B, numKvHeads, seqLen, HEAD_DIM}, result[1].shape(), "GQA present key shape");
   365	
   366	        assertFalse(result[0].isNaN().any());
   367	    }

   406	    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   407	    // MmulHelper mixed-type tests
   408	    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   409	
   410	    /**
   411	     * Matmul FLOAT [4,8] x HALF [8,3] → FLOAT [4,3] via SameDiff.
   412	     *
   413	     * The Java mmul() API enforces same-dtype at the Java layer, so mixed-type
   414	     * matmul must go through SameDiff which calls the C++ MmulHelper directly.
   415	     * The MmulHelper mixed-type safe path (added to fix buffer overruns from
   416	     * BLAS accessing FLOAT-sized elements in a HALF buffer) is exercised here.
   417	     *
   418	     * Correctness is verified against a FP32 reference: cast HALF to FLOAT, mmul.
   419	     */
   420	    @Test
   421	    public void testMmulMixedTypeFloat32xFloat16() {
   422	        int M = 4, K = 8, N = 3;
   423	        INDArray a = Nd4j.randn(DataType.FLOAT, M, K);
   424	        INDArray b = Nd4j.randn(DataType.HALF,  K, N);
   425	
   426	        // FP32 reference
   427	        INDArray bF32     = b.castTo(DataType.FLOAT);
   428	        INDArray expected = a.mmul(bF32);
   429	
   430	        // Mixed-type via SameDiff (calls C++ MmulHelper)
   431	        SameDiff sd = SameDiff.create();
   432	        SDVariable aVar = sd.placeHolder("a", DataType.FLOAT, M, K);
   433	        SDVariable bVar = sd.constant("b", b);
   434	        SDVariable result = sd.mmul("result", aVar, bVar);
   435	
   436	        Map<String, INDArray> out = sd.output(Map.of("a", a), "result");
   437	        INDArray actual = out.get("result").castTo(DataType.FLOAT);
   438	
   439	        assertArrayEquals(new long[]{M, N}, actual.shape(),
   440	                "mixed-type mmul output shape must be [M,N]");
   441	        assertFalse(actual.isNaN().any(),      "mixed-type mmul must not contain NaN");
   442	        assertFalse(actual.isInfinite().any(), "mixed-type mmul must not contain Inf");
   443	
   444	        // FP16 weight quantization introduces ~1% error — allow 0.5 absolute tolerance
   445	        double maxErr = expected.sub(actual).amaxNumber().doubleValue();
   446	        assertTrue(maxErr < 0.5,
   447	                "FLOAT x HALF mmul max error " + maxErr + " exceeds tolerance 0.5");
   448	
   449	        // Result must not be all zeros
   450	        double absSum = actual.amaxNumber().doubleValue();
   451	        assertTrue(absSum > 1e-6, "mixed-type mmul result must not be all zeros");
   452	
   453	        sd.close();
   454	    }

  1877	    /**
  1878	     * Scatter a present KV [batch, heads, 1, dim] into a static buffer at cachePos.
  1879	     */
  1880	    private static void scatterKvToStatic(INDArray presentKv, INDArray staticBuf, long cachePos) {
  1881	        // presentKv shape: [batch, heads, seqLen, dim] — take last position
  1882	        long seqLen = presentKv.shape()[2];
  1883	        INDArray slice = presentKv.get(
  1884	                NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
  1885	                NDArrayIndex.point(seqLen - 1), NDArrayIndex.all());
  1886	        staticBuf.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
  1887	                NDArrayIndex.point(cachePos), NDArrayIndex.all()).assign(slice);
  1888	    }

  1890	    /**
  1891	     * Resolve an external input index by name from the plan executor.
  1892	     */
  1893	    private static int resolveExtInputIdx(DynamicShapePlanExecutor executor, String name) {
  1894	        if (name == null || executor == null) return -1;
  1895	        return executor.findExternalInputIndex(name);

  1897	
  1898	    /**
  1899	     * Resolve an output index by name from the plan executor.

  1901	    private static int resolveOutputIdx(DynamicShapePlanExecutor executor, String name) {
  1902	        if (name == null || executor == null) return -1;
  1903	        return executor.findOutputIndex(name);
  1904	    }
  1905	
  1906	    /**
  1907	     * Pad a KV cache tensor from [batch, heads, seqLen, dim] to [batch, heads, maxKvLen, dim].
  1908	     */
  1909	    private static INDArray padKvToStaticSize(INDArray presentKv, long maxKvLen) {
  1910	        long[] shape = presentKv.shape();
  1911	        long batch = shape[0], heads = shape[1], seqLen = shape[2], dim = shape[3];
  1912	        if (seqLen >= maxKvLen) {
  1913	            return presentKv.dup();
  1914	        }
  1915	        INDArray padding = Nd4j.zeros(presentKv.dataType(), batch, heads, maxKvLen - seqLen, dim);
  1916	        INDArray result = Nd4j.concat(2, presentKv, padding);
  1917	        padding.close();
  1918	        return result;
  1919	    }

A few issues are now clear enough for review: the CUDA KV validation still reads `planOutputs[kvOutIdx]` before checking `kvOutIdx`, and the mixed GEMV fix currently only fixes the HALF/FLOAT cases, not the BF16 branches it claims to cover. I’m doing one more pass over changed-call coverage so the final review separates must-fix correctness from risk/coverage gaps.

    23	void NDArray::syncToDevice() {
    24	  if(_buffer == nullptr) return;
    25	
    26	  auto currentDeviceId = AffinityManager::currentDeviceId();
    27	  auto bufferDeviceId = _buffer->deviceId();
    28	
    29	  if (currentDeviceId != _deviceId || currentDeviceId != bufferDeviceId) {
    30	    const_cast<NDArray*>(this)->setShapeInfo(this->shapeInfo());
    31	    _buffer->migrate();
    32	    _deviceId = currentDeviceId;
    33	  }
    34	
    35	  _buffer->syncToSpecial();
    36	}
    37	
    38	void NDArray::syncToHost() { if(_buffer != nullptr) _buffer->syncToPrimary(getContext()); }
    39	void NDArray::forceSyncToHost() { if(_buffer != nullptr) _buffer->syncToPrimary(getContext(), true); }
    40	void NDArray::tickWriteHost() { if(_buffer != nullptr) _buffer->writePrimary(); }
    41	void NDArray::tickWriteDevice() { if(_buffer != nullptr) _buffer->writeSpecial(); }
    42	void NDArray::tickReadHost() { if(_buffer != nullptr) _buffer->readPrimary(); }
    43	void NDArray::tickReadDevice() { if(_buffer != nullptr) _buffer->readSpecial(); }
    44	
    45	void NDArray::tickBothActual() {
    46	  if(_buffer == nullptr) return;
    47	  _buffer->writePrimary();
    48	  _buffer->readSpecial();
    49	}
    50	
    51	bool NDArray::isActualOnHostSide() { return _buffer == nullptr ? true : _buffer->isPrimaryActual(); }
    52	bool NDArray::isActualOnDeviceSide() { return _buffer == nullptr ? true : _buffer->isSpecialActual(); }
    53	
    54	void NDArray::makeBothBuffersActual() {
    55	  if (!isActualOnHostSide()) syncToHost();
    56	  if (!isActualOnDeviceSide()) syncToDevice();
    57	}

    59	void NDArray::synchronize(const char* msg) {

   129	    public static Map<String, INDArray> buildDecoderInputMap(

   144

   147	                decoderInputMap.put(inputName, embeddings);

   153	                        usingStaticKv, reusableInputs, maxKvLen, currentSeqLen, cachePos, pastSeqLen);
   154	            } else if (ioConfig.isInputIds(inputName)) {
   155	                decoderInputMap.put(inputName, inputIds);
   156	            } else if (ioConfig.isPositionIds(inputName)) {
   157	                buildPositionIds(decoderInputMap, inputName, canReuse, reusableInputs,
   158	                        pastSeqLen, currentSeqLen);
   159	            } else if (ioConfig.isKvCacheInput(inputName)) {
   160	                buildKvCacheInput(decoderInputMap, inputName, decoder, usingStaticKv, usePadded,
   161	                        staticKvBuffers, cachePos, hiddenSize);
   162	            } else if (ioConfig.isEncoderHiddenStates(inputName)) {
   163	                if (encoderOutputs != null) {
   164	                    decoderInputMap.put(inputName, encoderOutputs);
   165	                }
   166	            } else if (ioConfig.isEncoderAttentionMask(inputName)) {
   167	                if (encoderAttentionMask != null) {

   204	    /**
   205	     * Keep internal decoder inputs phase-aligned with the per-step arrays built above.
   206	     */
   207	    public static void associateInternalModelInputs(ModelIOConfig ioConfig,
   208	                                                    List<String> materializedInputNames,
   209	                                                    SameDiff decoder,
   210	                                                    Map<String, INDArray> decoderInputMap) {
   211	        if (decoder == null || decoderInputMap == null || decoderInputMap.isEmpty()) {
   212	            return;
   213	        }
   214	
   215	        List<String> externalInputNames = decoder.externalInputs();
   216	        for (String inputName : materializedInputNames) {
   217	            if (inputName == null || !decoder.hasVariable(inputName)) {
   218	                continue;
   219	            }
   220	            INDArray arr = decoderInputMap.get(inputName);
   221	            if (arr != null && !externalInputNames.contains(inputName)) {
   222	                // Only associate with VARIABLE or CONSTANT type SDVariables.
   223	                // ARRAY-type variables are computed intermediates — calling
   224	                // associateArrayWithVariable on them throws UnsupportedOperationException.
   225	                SDVariable sdVar = decoder.getVariable(inputName);
   226	                if (sdVar != null && sdVar.getVariableType() != VariableType.ARRAY) {
   227	                    decoder.associateArrayWithVariable(arr, inputName);
   228	                }
   229	            }
   230	        }

   241	            long totalSeqLen = maxKvLen + currentSeqLen;
   242	            if (canReuse && reusableInputs.containsKey(inputName)) {
   243	                INDArray mask = reusableInputs.get(inputName);
   244	                // Delta update: previous step already set [0..cachePos-2]=1 and
   245	                // [totalSeqLen-1]=1.  Just mark the newly cached position.
   246	                if (cachePos > 0) {
   247	                    mask.putScalar(new long[]{0, cachePos - 1}, 1);
   248	                }
   249	                decoderInputMap.put(inputName, mask);
   250	            } else {
   251	                INDArray mask = Nd4j.zeros(DataType.LONG, 1, totalSeqLen);
   252	                if (cachePos > 0) {
   253	                    mask.get(NDArrayIndex.point(0), NDArrayIndex.interval(0, cachePos)).assign(1);
   254	                }
   255	                mask.putScalar(0, totalSeqLen - 1, 1);
   256	                decoderInputMap.put(inputName, mask);
   257	                if (canReuse) reusableInputs.put(inputName, mask);
   258	            }
   259	        } else if (usingStaticKv) {
   260	            long totalSeqLen = cachePos + currentSeqLen;
   261	            decoderInputMap.put(inputName, Nd4j.ones(DataType.LONG, 1, totalSeqLen));
   262	        } else {
   263	            long totalSeqLen = pastSeqLen + currentSeqLen;
   264	            decoderInputMap.put(inputName, Nd4j.ones(DataType.LONG, 1, totalSeqLen));
   265	        }
   266	    }
   267	
   268	    private static void buildCausalMask(ModelIOConfig ioConfig,
   269	                                         Map<String, INDArray> decoderInputMap, String inputName,
   270	                                         boolean canReuse, boolean usePadded, boolean usingStaticKv,
   271	                                         Map<String, INDArray> reusableInputs,
   272	                                         long maxKvLen, long currentSeqLen, long cachePos, long pastSeqLen) {
   273	        if (usePadded) {
   274	            long totalSeqLen = maxKvLen + currentSeqLen;
   275	            INDArray causalMask;
   276	            boolean reused = false;
   277	            if (canReuse && reusableInputs.containsKey(inputName)) {
   278	                causalMask = reusableInputs.get(inputName);
   279	                reused = true;
   280	            } else {
   281	                causalMask = Nd4j.zeros(DataType.FLOAT, 1, 1, currentSeqLen, totalSeqLen);
   282	                if (canReuse) reusableInputs.put(inputName, causalMask);
   283	            }
   284	            if (currentSeqLen == 1) {
   285	                if (reused) {
   286	                    // Delta update: the buffer already has the correct state from the
   287	                    // previous step — positions [0..cachePos-1] = 0.0f and
   288	                    // [cachePos..totalSeqLen-1] = MASK_FILL.  Just unmask position cachePos.
   289	                    causalMask.putScalar(new long[]{0, 0, 0, cachePos}, 0.0f);
   290	                } else {
   291	                    // First call: full fill then unmask prefix.
   292	                    causalMask.assign(ModelIOConfig.MASK_FILL);
   293	                    if (cachePos + 1 > 0) {
   294	                        causalMask.get(NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(), NDArrayIndex.all(),
   295	                                NDArrayIndex.interval(0, cachePos + 1)).assign(0.0f);
   296	                    }
   297	                }
   298	            } else {
   299	                causalMask.assign(ModelIOConfig.buildCausalMask(currentSeqLen, totalSeqLen));
   300	            }
   301	            decoderInputMap.put(inputName, causalMask);
   302	        } else if (usingStaticKv) {
   303	            long totalSeqLen = cachePos + currentSeqLen;
   304	            decoderInputMap.put(inputName, ModelIOConfig.buildCausalMask(currentSeqLen, totalSeqLen));
   305	        } else {
   306	            long totalSeqLen = pastSeqLen + currentSeqLen;
   307	            decoderInputMap.put(inputName, ModelIOConfig.buildCausalMask(currentSeqLen, totalSeqLen));
   308	        }
   309	    }

   311	    private static void buildPositionIds(Map<String, INDArray> decoderInputMap, String inputName,
   312	                                          boolean canReuse, Map<String, INDArray> reusableInputs,
   313	                                          long pastSeqLen, long currentSeqLen) {
   314	        if (canReuse && reusableInputs.containsKey(inputName)) {
   315	            INDArray posIds = reusableInputs.get(inputName);
   316	            writePositionIds(posIds, pastSeqLen, currentSeqLen);
   317	            decoderInputMap.put(inputName, posIds);
   318	        } else {
   319	            INDArray posIds = Nd4j.create(DataType.LONG, 1, currentSeqLen);
   320	            writePositionIds(posIds, pastSeqLen, currentSeqLen);
   321	            decoderInputMap.put(inputName, posIds);
   322	            if (canReuse) reusableInputs.put(inputName, posIds);
   323	        }
   324	    }

codex
**Critical**
- [autoregressive_decode.cu](/home/agibsonccc/Documents/GitHub/deeplearning4j/libnd4j/include/ops/declarable/helpers/cuda/autoregressive_decode.cu:834): CUDA still dereferences `planOutputs[kvOutIdx]` before validating `kvOutIdx`. The CPU path validates first. Move `presentKv = planOutputs[kvOutIdx]` below the `REQUIRE_TRUE(kvOutIdx >= 0 && kvOutIdx < numPlanOutputs, ...)`, otherwise a bad output index can segfault before the new always-on validation runs.

- [autoregressive_decode.cu](/home/agibsonccc/Documents/GitHub/deeplearning4j/libnd4j/include/ops/declarable/helpers/cuda/autoregressive_decode.cu:520): the new “pre-unmask current position” logic looks wrong for SmolDocling’s standard concat mode. With `cachePosition=null`, current K/V is appended after the static padded KV buffer, so pre-unmasking `currentPosition` exposes an unwritten static slot; the appended current token should already be represented by the final padded/query position. Gate this pre-unmask behavior on in-place KV/cache-position mode. The CPU path has the same issue at [autoregressive_decode.cpp](/home/agibsonccc/Documents/GitHub/deeplearning4j/libnd4j/include/ops/declarable/helpers/cpu/autoregressive_decode.cpp:306).

- [MmulHelper.cu](/home/agibsonccc/Documents/GitHub/deeplearning4j/libnd4j/include/helpers/cuda/MmulHelper.cu:1162): the mixed `mmulMxV` fix claims BF16 support but does not actually route BF16 through a valid path. `BFLOAT16 + FLOAT32` casts `X`, then falls through to `usualGemv` using the original `A`/`X` pointers and original `xType`, so it can still reinterpret BF16 memory as FLOAT32. Either remove the BF16 branches or add a real BF16 GEMV path using `effA`/`effX` and the correct dispatch.

**Important**
- [autoregressive_decode.cu](/home/agibsonccc/Documents/GitHub/deeplearning4j/libnd4j/include/ops/declarable/helpers/cuda/autoregressive_decode.cu:637): the CUDA state-copy guard compares `srcDb->getLenInBytes() > dstDb->getLenInBytes()`, but the actual copy size is `bytes`. For an overrun guard, check `bytes <= srcDb->getLenInBytes()` and `bytes <= dstDb->getLenInBytes()`. Comparing source capacity to destination capacity can skip valid copies when source has max allocation, and it does not directly validate the requested copy.

- [onnx_multi_head_attention.cpp](/home/agibsonccc/Documents/GitHub/deeplearning4j/libnd4j/include/ops/declarable/generic/nn/onnx_multi_head_attention.cpp:267): the restored `syncToDevice()` calls are defensible as a coherence repair, but the comment says source views may be host-actual while the code syncs destination slices after assignment. If the actual bug is stale source device data, this does not fix it. Add a targeted test with host-actual static KV inputs and non-contiguous/permuted views; if that reproduces, sync/prepare the source views before assignment as well.

- No tests are included in the current diff. At minimum, add coverage for: standard concat with padded static KV and no `cache_position`; non-contiguous output MHA copy-back; mixed `mmulMxV` HALF/FLOAT and BF16/FLOAT; and invalid KV output index validation order.

**Minor**
- [GenerationPipeline.java](/home/agibsonccc/Documents/GitHub/deeplearning4j/nd4j/samediff-llm/src/main/java/org/eclipse/deeplearning4j/llm/generation/GenerationPipeline.java:1509): the causal-mask `<= prefillSeqLen` change also assumes current KV lives at `prefillSeqLen`. That is correct for in-place KV, but not obviously correct for standard padded concat where current KV is appended at `maxKvLen`. SmolDocling has `causalMask=null`, so this probably does not explain its regression, but the code should document or gate the mode.

**Positive**
- Restoring `AttentionWorkspace + nullify + assign` in [onnx_multi_head_attention.cpp](/home/agibsonccc/Documents/GitHub/deeplearning4j/libnd4j/include/ops/declarable/generic/nn/onnx_multi_head_attention.cpp:372) is the safer fix. A direct-write version could work only with a hard stride-based C-contiguity check, explicit zeroing, and fallback copy-back tests. Given DSP plan allocations and reshape-copy ambiguity, the workspace path is the right correctness-first move.

Answers to the specific questions: `REQUIRE_TRUE` every step is not a real performance concern; the macro only evaluates the diagnostic arguments on failure, and the conditions are O(1). The `logitsName` default change is unlikely to affect SmolDocling when `GenerationPipeline.create()` is used because it validates/discovers outputs, but it is still a risky default for any path that bypasses discovery. Other credible regression candidates are the `mmulMxV` mixed-dtype changes and DSP steady-state external-input sync changes, not just MHA.
